Existing implicit methods often suffer from local minima under noisy supervision and lack physical interpretability, while explicit approaches rely on predefined constitutive equations, limiting generalizability and becoming unstable in monocular settings. To address these challenges, our framework unifies LoRA-based adaptation with two key alignment modules. First, we propose Rank-based Depth-Geometric Anchors (RDGA) to establish robust geometric constraints from monocular dynamic observations via scale-invariant rank-based depth alignment, reducing the reliance on unreliable pixel-level color supervision. Second, a Constitutive Prior Regularizer (CPR) integrates classical constitutive models as soft differentiable priors, regularizing the optimization while preserving the flexibility of implicit modeling---even when the actual material is absent from the hypotheses. Extensive experiments on synthetic, real-to-sim, and real-world datasets demonstrate that GCA outperforms existing methods, achieving 48% lower Chamfer Distance than the strongest baseline on synthetic benchmarks while remaining robust under monocular supervision.

</details>


### [70] [Vehicle speed dataset for the major European road network derived from Sentinel-2 imagery, 2022-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22116)
*Maciej Adamiak,Sascha Fendrich,Julian Psotta,Alexander Zipf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于哨兵‑2多光谱波段微秒级行扫时延，自动从欧洲E级公路光学影像中提取单车轨迹并估算车速，生成2022–2026年车辆速度与轨迹GeoPackage数据集，可用于交通模式与道路属性校准研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏跨国、连续、统一的方法来在大陆尺度观测道路交通速度与行为；传统浮动车/感应线圈数据存在覆盖与获取限制。利用哨兵‑2的多谱段时延提供了在无专用传感器条件下被动测速的新机会。

Method: 利用Sentinel‑2推扫相机蓝、绿、红三波段获取时间存在已知短延迟：同一移动车辆在三波段位置发生可预测位移，形成“移动回波”。管线包括：1) 从L2A光学影像检测各波段位移峰；2) 通过预测‑匹配将峰连接为单车轨迹；3) 依据公开的跨波段时间延迟将像元位移换算为地面速度与航向；4) 输出每车记录（轨迹几何、各通道位移/航向、质量指标、速度、时间戳、源产品ID）。

Result: 产生2022–2026年覆盖欧洲E-road（高速、主干、一级、二级路）的逐车速度观测数据集，单位为一车一记录，包含完整几何与元数据，分发为GeoPackage文件。

Conclusion: 该数据集在大陆尺度提供被动遥感逐车速度测量，可支撑交通模式分析、速度行为研究、运输建模与道路网络属性标定，证明多谱段时延可行且具规模化潜力。

Abstract: The dataset provides individual vehicle speed observations on European E-roads: motorways, trunk roads, primary and secondary roads, as tagged in OpenStreetMap as e-road, for the years 2022-2026. Speeds are derived from Copernicus Sentinel-2 Level-2A satellite optical imagery using a processing pipeline that exploits the short, well-characterized acquisition delays between the blue (B02_10m), green (B03_10m), and red bands (B04_10m) of the Sentinel-2 push-broom instrument. A moving vehicle appears at slightly displaced positions in the three bands, forming a moving echo. The detected displaced intensity peaks are linked into per-vehicle trajectories through a prediction-and-matching procedure. The resulting displacements are converted into ground speeds using publicly accessible inter-band time delays. Each record contains the trajectory geometry, per-channel displacements and headings, internal quality indicators, the estimated speed, the acquisition timestamp, and the source Sentinel-2 product identifier. The dataset is distributed as GeoPackage files, with one record per detected vehicle, and can support studies of traffic patterns, speed behavior, transport modeling, and the calibration of road network attributes at a continental scale.

</details>


### [71] [TRACE: Artifact-Robust Statistical Shape Modeling from Imperfect Surface Scans - A Case Study in Craniosynostosis 3D Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22131)
*Sanjay Bhandari,Nawazish Khan,Alzbeta Novotna,Tiffany Jeong,Loretta Bowman,Michael Hernandez,Tobi Somorin,Viraj Govani,Jesse Goldstein,Shireen Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，从含噪原始临床3D头部照片中，无监督地建立头颅统计形状模型；通过稀疏解剖对应点、表面感知级联变形与薄板样条，将干净模板变形为个体重建，保持解剖对应并抑制伪影，跨多种点云编码骨干均提升采样、拓扑与SSM质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有颅缝早闭严重度评估依赖CT或高度清洗的3D照片，存在辐射风险或重度预处理需求；原始临床3D照片虽可重复且无辐射，但常含肩手头发衣物与扫描噪声、边界缺失，导致对应关系受损，难以直接构建统计形状模型。

Method: 提出TRACE（Template-constrained Robust Artifact-aware Correspondence Estimation）：1）从原始点云预测稀疏、解剖对应的头表控制点；2）采用由粗到细的Surface-Aware Deformation级联精炼对应；3）利用薄板样条（TPS）将干净模板网格变形为个体头部重建；4）对应模块与点云编码器解耦，可与PointNet、DGCNN、Point Transformer V3等骨干替换组合；通过模板约束使致密对应集中在临床相关头部解剖并抑制非头部伪影。

Result: 在多种编码骨干下，TRACE相较以往SSM方法显著提高表面采样质量、保持网格拓扑一致性，并提升形状模型质量；能稳健处理包含伪影与不完整边界的临床3D头部照片。

Conclusion: TRACE为基于临床3D照片的颅缝早闭形状分析提供可扩展、无监督且对伪影鲁棒的基础流程；当提供合适的干净模板时，该框架可推广至其他被伪影污染的表面扫描场景。

Abstract: Craniosynostosis severity analysis increasingly relies on statistical shape models (SSMs) to quantify cranial morphology, but most existing workflows depend on computed tomography or heavily curated three-dimensional (3D) photographs. Raw clinical 3D photographs provide a radiation-free and repeatable alternative, yet often contain shoulders, hands, hair, clothing, scanner noise, and incomplete boundaries that corrupt correspondences. We introduce the Template-constrained Robust Artifact-aware Correspondence Estimation (TRACE) framework, an unsupervised method for constructing SSMs directly from artifact-contaminated clinical 3D head photographs. TRACE predicts sparse anatomically corresponding head-surface control points from the raw point cloud, refines them through a coarse-to-fine Surface-Aware Deformation cascade, and uses thin-plate spline warping to deform a clean template mesh into a subject-specific head reconstruction. This template-constrained formulation keeps dense correspondences on clinically relevant head anatomy while suppressing non-head artifacts. The correspondence module is decoupled from the point-cloud encoder, enabling the same deformation pipeline to be paired with different backbones, including PointNet, DGCNN, and Point Transformer V3. Across all backbones, TRACE substantially improves surface sampling, topology preservation, and shape-model quality over prior SSM methods, providing a scalable foundation for photograph-based craniosynostosis shape analysis and a framework that may extend to other artifact-contaminated surface scans when an appropriate clean template is available.

</details>


### [72] [When Does Visual Generation Help Visual Understanding in Unified Multimodal Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22174)
*Yubo Zhu,Zhehan Kan,Jingyi Yang,Miaolin Chen,Jinbo Xing,Kai Zhu,Zijian Wang,Sheng Zhong,Wei Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGAU-Diag评估框架，专门诊断“视觉生成是否能提升视觉理解”。结果：生成的可视化在简单样本上有助益，难度增加时易失效；瓶颈主要在理解侧而非生成侧；有效生成应直击理解瓶颈，且生成辅助经历噪声→误导→有益三阶段转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMM同时做视觉理解与生成，但现有评测把任务难度、推理范式与生成-理解闭环混在一起，导致“生成是否帮理解”答案不清。需要一个可分层、可跨范式、可隔离误差来源的细粒度诊断基准。

Method: 构建VGAU-Diag：1) 按样本难度分层；2) 统一评估多种推理范式（如直接回答、逐步推理、工具使用等）；3) 设计Oracle-Assisted Reference Protocols，将理想的视觉生成（或理想的理解信号）注入，切断闭环，定位是生成侧还是理解侧的瓶颈；4) 分析模型在有/无生成辅助下的表现与误差类型。

Result: 实验证明：生成的视觉辅助在“易—中等”实例能提升，但在高复杂度推理时不可靠；在给到“忠实/理想”的视觉辅助下，模型仍常失败，说明主要短板在视觉理解与利用能力；进一步发现盲目增加生成步骤无益，需面向理解瓶颈定向生成；观察到辅助质量从任务无关噪声→看似合理但误导→真正有用的三阶段转变。

Conclusion: 要让生成真正助力理解，需：1) 针对理解瓶颈定制化生成；2) 减少无关或误导性内容；3) 在高复杂度任务上优先强化理解与信息利用能力。VGAU-Diag为未来UMM设计与评测提供可操作的诊断工具与方向。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) can perform both understanding and generation, raising a central question: can visual generation improve understanding? Existing evaluations provide mixed evidence, but confound task difficulty, reasoning paradigms, and the closed-loop interaction between generation and understanding. We introduce VGAU-Diag, a fine-grained evaluation framework for vision generation-assisted understanding. It stratifies samples by difficulty, enables unified evaluation of multiple reasoning paradigms, and uses Oracle-Assisted Reference Protocols. Our analysis shows that generated visual aids help on easier instances but become unreliable as reasoning complexity increases. Oracle-assisted diagnosis further reveals that the main bottleneck often lies on the visual-understanding side rather than the visual-generation side, as current UMMs struggle to leverage even faithful visual aids. We also show that effective visual generation should target visual-understanding bottlenecks rather than add more reasoning steps, and identify a three-stage transition from task-irrelevant noise, to misleading plausible guidance, and finally to useful assistance. These findings would be useful to guide the development of better UMMs.The code is available at https://github.com/zyb1029/VGAU-Diag.

</details>


### [73] [VERDICT: Agreement Beats Pixel-Space Verification in Real-Document OCSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22183)
*Yani Guan,Dengpan Dong,Shuang Luo,Zi Wei,Joah Han,Dan Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Qichao Hu,Kang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究比较三类无标签置信信号以自动甄别OCSR结果的可靠性，发现“多模型一致性”显著优于“重渲染相似度”，据此提出VERDICT流程，在多数据集与真实论文图中实现高精度筛选与标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模化学数据集需要从文献化学结构图中自动提取SMILES，但缺少真值时难以判断预测是否可靠。需要找到无需标签即可评估OCSR结果可信度的有效指标，并验证其在真实与多源数据上的泛化。

Method: 在含真值的263张ACS分子图上比较三类无标签信号：模型自信度、像素级重渲染与输入相似度、不同架构识别器间的一致性。评估AUROC与操作点（如二/三/四一致规则），并在CLEF-IP、UOB、USPTO复现实验。设计“物质过滤器”剔除通配与R基碎片导致的虚假一致。将最佳策略整合为VERDICT流程并应用于PMC OA规模数据，进行化学家双盲抽样评估精度。

Result: 像素重渲染几乎等同随机（AUROC 0.547），且即使调参也大幅降低有效标签率；四模型一致性AUROC达0.916。二-of-四规则：接受81.7%图，精度88.8%；三-of-四：接受52.1%，精度98.5%。该趋势在CLEF-IP、UOB、USPTO复现。物质过滤器移除2,193个通配/R基的假一致，之后三-of-四拒绝全部68个通用（generic）图。应用PMC后获得6,146个结构标签（4,833分子）；化学家复核两批各200条：三-of-四精度0.995、二-of-四精度0.958。

Conclusion: 重渲染相似度在真实图像上并不可靠，多模型一致性是强而稳健的无标签可靠性信号。VERDICT将该原则工程化，可在大规模文献中产出高精度结构标签，支撑多模态分子数据库与基于图像的分子检索。

Abstract: Optical Chemical Structure Recognition (OCSR) converts 2D molecular depictions in the published literature into SMILES, and is increasingly important for constructing large-scale chemical training datasets. Automation at that scale requires identifying unreliable predictions in the absence of ground truth. Three families of label-free signals were compared on $263$ ACS journal depictions with verified ground truth: model confidence, re-rendering similarity, and agreement among recognizers. Pixel-space re-rendering performed little better than chance (AUROC $0.547$, $95\%$ CI $[0.465,0.629]$), and an oracle-tuned threshold on it reduced correct labels per image from $0.745$ to $0.205$. Agreement among four architecturally distinct recognizers instead reached an AUROC of $0.916$ ($[0.880,0.952]$). The two-of-four rule accepted $81.7\%$ of images at $88.8\%$ precision, the three-of-four rule $52.1\%$ at $98.5\%$. The same pattern held on CLEF-IP, UOB, and USPTO. This distinction is obscured on synthetic benchmarks, where re-rendered predictions naturally resemble their inputs. A substance filter removed $2{,}193$ false agreements on wildcards and R-group fragments, after which the three-of-four rule rejected all $68$ generic depictions. VERDICT was then applied to PMC Open Access, producing $6{,}146$ structure labels for $4{,}833$ molecules; chemist adjudication of $400$ released labels in two independent samples yielded precisions of $0.995$ for the three-of-four tier and $0.958$ for the two-of-four tier. VERDICT therefore enables validated labels for multimodal molecular databases linking structure images, machine-readable representations, and source-publication information. In SES AI's Molecular Universe platform, VERDICT further serves as an image-based interface for searching and retrieving molecular records.

</details>


### [74] [SAM3Dual: A 3rd Place Solution to the MOSEv2 Track, 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22193)
*JeongRae Kim,Chaehyun Kim,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM3Dual 是一个对预训练 SAM 3 进行“推理期记忆重组”的无训练方法：用短期与长期两路显式记忆，并以确定性时序融合且受上一帧置信度调节，在不改动任何参数的前提下，在 MOSEv2 赛道取得 J&F 64.37、总排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时视频分割需要在长时间跨度内保持对象一致性与鲁棒性；传统做法依赖训练或在线更新，成本高、泛化差。作者想证明：仅通过在推理阶段重构时间记忆组织与调度，无需微调即可获得有竞争力的长期 VOS 性能。

Method: 在预训练 SAM 3 上新增“训练期零改动、推理期扩展”的双分支记忆：短期分支存最近帧，长期分支以间隔采样汇聚历史表示；两路记忆以确定性的与序列位置相关的融合策略组合，并由上一帧目标置信度进行保守调制；所有 SAM 3 参数冻结，无额外训练/微调/在线优化。

Result: 在 ECCV 2026 LSVOS 挑战 MOSEv2 赛道，官方 J&F=64.37，排名第三。

Conclusion: 仅在推理期重组时间记忆即可在长期视频分割上取得有竞争力的结果，显示了保持预训练模型不变、通过记忆调度与融合策略提升性能的潜力。

Abstract: We present SAM3Dual, our third-place solution to the MOSEv2 track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge at ECCV 2026. SAM3Dual is a training-free inference extension of pretrained SAM 3 that explicitly separates temporal memory into a short-term branch for recent observations and a long-term branch for interval-sampled historical representations. The two memory responses are combined using a deterministic sequence-relative fusion schedule and conservatively modulated by the previous-frame object confidence. All pretrained SAM 3 parameters remain frozen, requiring no task-specific training, fine-tuning, test-time training, or online parameter optimization. The complete system achieved an official J&F score of 64.37 and ranked third in the MOSEv2 track. This result highlights the potential of reorganizing temporal memory entirely at inference time to obtain competitive long-term VOS performance while preserving the pretrained model.

</details>


### [75] [UR$^{2}$-MLLM: Uncertainty-aware Revisit Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22217)
*Yucheng Chen,Yang Yu,Jiazhou Zhou,Yufei Shi,Yongying Lan,Yichi Zhang,Liyi Li,Si Yong Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UR²-MLLM：在放射科报告生成中，基于不确定性感知的动态“回访”可疑图像区域，提高临床一致性与SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRG的“带图思考”多模态LLM仅提供静态证据，缺少像放射科医师那样对不确定观测进行动态回访，导致推理不够可靠、与解剖结构对齐不足。

Method: 1) 不确定性感知：在不确定性标注数据上训练，使模型识别不确定区域；2) 构造多模态推理轨迹数据并引入detect-and-copy机制，指导何时/何处回访；3) 通过视觉定位奖励进行强化学习，使回访区域与相应解剖结构对齐。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR与IU-Xray上取得SOTA，证明动态基于不确定性的回访推理可提升报告生成质量与临床对齐度。

Conclusion: 将不确定性感知与动态回访融入RRG推理流程，可更可靠地整合视觉证据并对齐解剖结构，显著提升临床有效性与性能。

Abstract: Radiologists generate diagnostic reports through iterative and selective revisiting of suspicious regions to refine their interpretations. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for radiology report generation (RRG) have shifted from text-only reasoning toward a ``Thinking-with-Images'' paradigm, incorporating visual evidence into the reasoning process. However, existing methods provide static visual evidence without a dynamic revisit mechanism during reasoning, neglecting how radiologists re-examine uncertain observations. To this end, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Revisit Reasoning MLLM (UR$^{2}$-MLLM) framework that dynamically revisits uncertain regions during reasoning for RRG. UR$^{2}$-MLLM is first equipped with uncertainty perception by training on an uncertainty-aware dataset. We then construct a multimodal reasoning trajectory dataset together with a detect-and-copy mechanism, which guides when and where to revisit. Finally, a visual grounding reward refines this behavior through reinforcement learning, aligning the revisited regions with corresponding anatomical structures. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray show that UR$^{2}$-MLLM achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the value of uncertainty-aware visual revisit reasoning for reliable and clinically aligned report generation.

</details>


### [76] [Hyper^2: Unleashing Hyperbolic Geometry's Full Potential via Dual-Space Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22238)
*Guantian Zheng,Haiyang Xu,Tianyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出仅用双曲Chamfer损失(HyperbolicCD)带来有限提升，因为损失在欧式编码器中回传被“平均”掉，提出“跨几何失配”。作者给出两个与模型无关的可测指标，并提出Hyper^2：在编码器的注意力中加入同形的双曲距离偏置，同时使用双曲Chamfer损失、共享曲率α，实现编码器与损失的几何一致，显著提升点云补全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作把欧氏Chamfer替换成双曲形式(arcosh(1+α||x−y||^2))虽有收益，但不大。作者认为瓶颈不在损失本身，而在损失与编码器几何不一致：双曲损失通过欧氏编码器反传，位置相关的监督被链式法则“抹平”，导致监督利用率低。需要验证并解决这种跨几何失配。

Method: 1) 定义并实证两个模型无关指标：特征-损失相关系数r_FL与有效梯度利用率u_G，用于检测几何失配。2) 提出Hyper^2：在精炼注意力中加入与损失同构的双曲距离编码(将arcosh(1+α d^2)作为位置偏置)，并与双曲Chamfer损失共享同一曲率α，形成“编码器+损失”双空间一致。计算代价仅为对欧氏距离的标量非线性，复杂度O(N log N)，对SVDFormer仅增约1.6% FLOPs。

Result: 在SVDFormer上，仅用双曲损失或仅用双曲编码器偏置的单空间改动提升有限；二者同时使用(Hyper^2)显著提升：在ShapeNet-55上Chamfer下降22.9%，在21个未见类的ShapeNet-34上下降37.5%。指标方面，单空间配置下(r_FL,u_G)基本不变，而在Hyper^2下同时跃升至(0.95,87%)，表明监督有效传递大幅提高。

Conclusion: 点云补全中，单独引入双曲损失或双曲编码并不足以发挥优势；关键在于编码器与损失的几何一致。Hyper^2通过共享曲率的双曲距离编码+双曲Chamfer损失实现一致性，显著提升性能并验证跨几何失配假设。代码已开源。

Abstract: HyperbolicCD pioneered hyperbolic geometry for point cloud completion by replacing the Euclidean Chamfer distance with arcosh(1+alpha||x-y||^2), but the reported gains are modest (3-7% Chamfer reduction across SeedFormer, PointAttN and PMP-Net backbones on PCN and ShapeNet-55). We argue the bottleneck lies elsewhere: the loss is hyperbolic but the encoder it back-propagates through is Euclidean, so the position-dependent supervision of the loss is averaged away by the chain rule before it reaches the parameters. We call this a cross-geometry mismatch, and make it testable through two model-agnostic indicators, feature-loss correlation r_FL and effective gradient utilisation u_G. On an SVDFormer backbone trained with HyperbolicCD's loss alone we measure (r_FL, u_G) = (0.68, 39%). We propose Hyper^2, a dual-space consistency framework that extends HyperbolicCD by reusing the identical arcosh(1+alpha d^2) functional form as a positional bias on the refinement attention (a hyperbolic distance encoding), paired with HyperbolicCD's hyperbolic Chamfer loss under a single shared curvature alpha. Both operators are O(N log N) scalar non-linearities on Euclidean distances and together add only ~1.6% FLOPs over SVDFormer. Hyper^2 delivers -22.9% Chamfer on ShapeNet-55 over SVDFormer (well above the 13.2% linear sum of the -12.0% loss-only and -1.2% encoding-only single-space ablations) and -37.5% on the 21 unseen ShapeNet-34 categories. The two indicators remain essentially flat for any single-space configuration but jump together to (0.95, 87%) only when both encoder and loss are hyperbolic, supporting the claim that geometric consistency across encoder and loss, rather than either operator alone, is what enables hyperbolic supervision in point cloud completion. Code is available at https://github.com/Ethan-Zheng136/Hyper-2.

</details>


### [77] [Training-Free VLM Personalization via Calibrated Residual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22263)
*Jiaao Yu,Yujian Ma,Xianming Hu,Pengran Wang,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的“校准残差解码”框架，通过正向、反事实与空配置三种证据条件，对个性化信号的边际贡献进行显式估计，并用归一化熵进行不确定性校准，从而在不微调的情况下稳定提升多模态个性化理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接把用户档案/偏好/视觉参考注入推理阶段的个性化提示，往往无法确保模型真正依赖这些证据；模型输出会混合通用先验与个性化线索，导致高置信答案可能并非由个性化档案支撑，难以判断个性化是否真正生效。

Method: 在同一图像与问题下构造三种证据条件：正向用户档案（positive）、反事实档案（counterfactual）、空档案（empty）。以正向档案的预测为锚，利用三种条件之间的分数差异来估计个性化信号的边际贡献，并引入基于归一化熵的不确定性校准，按残差信号的可靠性自适应调节个性化增强强度。

Result: 在MMPB、YoLLaVA、MyVLM等基准上无需微调即可带来一致提升，尤其在对身份敏感的视觉个性化任务上效果显著；分析表明当对比个性化信号不确定时，熵校准可稳定残差解码。

Conclusion: 通过训练自由的残差估计与熵校准，能区分并放大由用户档案真正带来的贡献，减少通用先验的干扰，从而稳健提升个性化多模态理解性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models can be personalized in a training-free manner by directly providing user profiles, preferences, or visual references at inference time, without updating model parameters. However, direct personalized prompting does not guarantee that the model will reliably exploit such evidence. The predictive distribution under the positive user profile often mixes two sources: personalized signals genuinely supported by the current profile, and the model's generic visual or linguistic priors. As a result, from the positive-profile response alone, it is difficult to determine whether a high-confidence answer is supported by the user profile or merely reflects the model's default preference. To address this problem, we propose a training-free calibrated residual decoding framework. Given the same image and question, we construct three evidence conditions: a positive profile , a counterfactual profile , and an empty profile . Our method keeps the prediction under
  as the anchored base, and explicitly estimates the marginal contribution of personalization from score differences across the three conditions. We further introduce normalized-entropy-based uncertainty calibration, allowing the strength of personalized enhancement to adapt to the reliability of the residual signal. Experiments on MMPB, YoLLaVA, and MyVLM show that the proposed method improves personalized multimodal understanding without fine-tuning, with consistent gains on identity-sensitive visual personalization tasks. Additional analysis shows that entropy calibration stabilizes residual decoding when the contrastive personalization signal is uncertain.

</details>


### [78] [GAN-Diff : Coupling Pretrained WGAN-GP Features with Conditional Diffusion U-Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22272)
*Saif Ahmed,Ashadulla Hil Galib,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Ahmed Faizul Haque Dhrubo,Souvik Pramanik,Mohammad Abdul Qayum,Mohsin Sajjad,Mohammad Ashrafuzzaman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种混合式GAN引导扩散的图像复原框架：用冻结的WGAN-GP生成器的中间特征作为先验，通过跨注意力注入到扩散U-Net，在DDIM采样时保持不变；在CelebA上做高斯去噪与2×超分，显著优于退化输入（PSNR去噪+4.40 dB，超分+3.70 dB），并通过一系列训练/采样稳健性改进解决不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAN生成高效但复原质量/稳定性受限；扩散模型复原质量高但采样迭代慢且在条件较弱时可能不稳定。动机是利用GAN学到的丰富先验与扩散的稳定重建能力互补，用冻结GAN特征指导扩散，实现更稳、更优的图像复原。

Method: 1) 预训练WGAN-GP生成器并冻结；2) 抽取其多层中间特征作为“特征先验”；3) 在条件扩散U-Net中加入跨注意力模块，将GAN特征与扩散特征对齐融合；4) 采用DDIM进行推理，GAN分支在采样全程不更新；5) 针对不稳定源做工程性修正：平衡对抗学习率、合理扩散初始化、控制退化强度、参数指数滑动平均等。

Result: 在CelebA上两项任务：高斯去噪与2×超分。相较各自退化输入基线，PSNR分别提升4.40 dB与3.70 dB；视觉质量与稳定性均提升。

Conclusion: 冻结的GAN特征先验能有效引导扩散模型进行稳定、有效的图像复原；混合框架在噪声去除与低分辨率重建上取得一致改进，显示GAN先验与扩散推理的互补潜力。

Abstract: Generative adversarial networks (GANs) can provide efficient image generation, while diffusion models offer high-quality image restoration but require iterative sampling. This paper presents a hybrid GAN-guided diffusion framework that uses a pretrained Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty (WGAN-GP) as a feature prior for conditional diffusion-based image restoration. Intermediate features from the frozen WGAN-GP generator are incorporated into a diffusion U-Net through cross-attention and remain fixed during the DDIM sampling process. The framework is evaluated on two restoration tasks, Gaussian denoising and 2Xsuper-resolution, using CelebA face images. During development, several sources of instability were identified and addressed, including adversarial learning-rate imbalance, inappropriate diffusion initialization, excessive corruption, and insufficient parameter averaging. The resulting framework consistently improves the quality of both degraded and low-resolution images. In particular, it improves denoising performance by 4.40 dB in PSNR and super-resolution performance by 3.70 dB over their respective input baselines. These results demonstrate the potential of a frozen GAN feature prior to guide diffusion models toward stable and effective image restoration.

</details>


### [79] [OVIBench: Benchmarking Online Video Question Answering under Interruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22279)
*Naiming Liu,Zhiheng Wu,Shuning Wang,Tie Zhang,Bowen Liu,Tong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在线中断场景下的视频问答任务（OVQA-Interrupt）与首个基准OVIBench，涵盖取消、误触发、纠正三类中断，提供开放式与多选评测、离线仿真协议和多维指标；并给出用于微调的OVI-Train。实验显示基准能区分模型处理中断能力，纠正类最具挑战；在OVI-Train上微调显著提升成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答研究多为离线、单轮设定，忽视真实人机交互中用户在模型生成过程中打断的情况，缺乏系统化评测与数据，难以衡量和提升VLM的在线鲁棒交互能力。

Method: 1) 定义“可中断的在线视频问答”任务；2) 构建OVIBench，设计三类中断：取消、误触发、纠正；3) 设计统一时间轴下的离线仿真协议，模拟生成过程被插入的中断；4) 提供开放式与多选两种评测形式；5) 制定多维指标，联合评估中断理解与生成质量；6) 构建用于中断感知微调的数据集OVI-Train。

Result: OVIBench能有效拉开各VLM在中断处理上的差距，尤其在“纠正”请求跟随方面差异显著；使用OVI-Train进行微调的模型在OVIBench上取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 在线、可中断的视频问答是更贴近实用的评测场景；OVIBench与OVI-Train提供了标准化基准与数据，验证了中断建模与训练的有效性，将推动VLM在鲁棒交互与指令跟随方面的发展；相关资源将发布。

Abstract: Recent vision language models (VLMs) have achieved strong progress in video understanding. However, most existing video QA research and benchmarks still follow an offline, single-round paradigm, overlooking realistic interactions where users may interrupt the model during answer generation. To address this gap, we formulate the task of Online Video Question Answering under Interruption and introduce OVIBench, the first standardized benchmark for evaluating VLMs in this setting. OVIBench categorizes interruptions into three types: Cancellation, False Trigger, Correction and supports both open-ended and multiple-choice evaluations. To enable large-scale and reproducible testing, we develop an offline simulation protocol that reproduces interruption during generation under a unified temporal setup, together with a multi-dimensional metric suite for assessing interruption understanding and response generation. Experiments demonstrate that OVIBench effectively distinguishes models' interruption-handling abilities, especially in following correction requests. Finally, we construct a train set OVI-Train for interruption-aware fine-tuning. Models fine-tuned on this dataset achieve significant gains on OVIBench, validating the effectiveness of our benchmark and data design. OVIBench, OVI-Train, and the evaluation code will be released.

</details>


### [80] [DECO: Depth-Guided Co-Visibility Reasoning for Low-Altitude UAV Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22289)
*Yibin Ye,Xichao Teng,Shuo Chen,Xiaokai Song,Dongdong Guan,Qifeng Yu,Zhang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DECO：利用单目深度先验进行共可视性推理，筛选既显著又几何可见的关键点，显著提升低空无人机在无GNSS环境下的视觉定位与PnP求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机常需在无GNSS环境中定位。现有方法用UAV图像与正射参考图（卫星/航测）做关键点匹配并PnP求位姿，但正射图主要是顶视屋顶/地面，立面信息缺失，导致UAV图中大量显著但在参考图中不可见的关键点被误匹配，影响定位精度与稳定性。

Method: 提出DECO框架：1) 由单目深度先验估计局部表面几何，推断UAV图像与正射参考图的共可视区域；2) 设计几何-显著性耦合的共可视性评分，将几何共可视性与特征检测器的显著性联合，用于关键点排序与筛选；3) 在筛选后进行特征匹配并用PnP进行位姿求解。框架可与不同的深度模型、特征检测器与匹配器集成。

Result: 在广泛实验中，相比基线显著提升匹配质量与PnP定位精度与稳健性，在多种深度模型与特征/匹配器组合下均保持优越表现。

Conclusion: 利用深度先验进行共可视性推理与关键点重排序能有效过滤不可见/冗余关键点，显著提升低空UAV在正射参考图上的视觉定位；方法通用、可插拔，代码开源。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) increasingly require robust visual localization in GNSS-denied environments. A common solution estimates UAV poses by matching keypoints between UAV images and geo-tagged orthographic reference maps derived from satellite or aerial imagery, followed by Perspective-\(n\)-Point (PnP) pose solving. However, such reference maps mainly record top-down surfaces such as roofs and ground planes, while vertical structures such as facades and walls are often compressed or missing. Consequently, many visually distinctive keypoints in low-altitude UAV images have no valid counterparts in the reference map, leading to redundant matches and inaccurate pose estimation. To address this issue, we propose DECO, a DEpth-guided CO-visibility reasoning framework for low-altitude UAV visual localization. DECO uses monocular depth priors to infer local surface geometry and estimate co-visible regions between UAV images and the reference map. Based on this prior, a Geometry-Saliency Coupled Co-visibility Score is introduced to jointly consider geometric co-visibility and detector saliency for keypoint ranking. In this way, DECO retains keypoints that are both visually distinctive and geometrically co-visible, improving feature matching and PnP-based pose estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECO achieves superior localization performance and can be integrated with different depth models, feature detectors, and matchers. The source code will be available at https://github.com/UAV-AVL/DECO.

</details>


### [81] [Targeted Iterative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22299)
*Freddie Åström,Michael Felsberg,George Baravdish,Claes Lundström*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于应用驱动的“值域变换+线性扩散→原空间等效非线性扩散”的图像去噪方案，并优于现有非线性扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同应用（压缩、静态成像、医疗）对去噪的偏好差异很大，传统统一的非线性扩散难以同时兼顾，需有可按应用定制的去噪机制。

Method: 先根据具体应用定义一个合适的值域（特征/强度）变换，将图像映射到该空间内执行线性扩散，再映回原图像空间；从而在原空间中得到相当于特定非线性扩散的效果。

Result: 在给定的应用驱动变换空间中实施的线性扩散，在定性/定量比较中优于现有非线性扩散技术。

Conclusion: 通过应用特定的值域变换把去噪问题转化为线性扩散，可获得比传统非线性扩散更佳的性能，表明“变换域线性、原域非线性”的框架具有通用性与优势。

Abstract: The assessment of image denoising results depends on the respective application area, i.e. image compression, still-image acquisition, and medical images require entirely different behavior of the applied denoising method. In this paper we propose a novel, nonlinear diffusion scheme that is derived from a linear diffusion process in a value space determined by the application. We show that application-driven linear diffusion in the transformed space compares favorably with existing nonlinear diffusion techniques.

</details>


### [82] [Self-Calibrating Dense Displacement Fields for Reliable Co-Registration of Large Optical Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22300)
*Shoukun Sun,Zhe Wang,Sanaz Salati,Jiyin Zhang,Hui Wang,Xiaogang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCDF提出一种无需训练、无需GPU的自校准稠密位移场配准方法，在多源/多时相光学遥感图像上稳定实现亚像素精度，并显著优于多种经典与零样本学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多时相/多传感器光学卫星图像配准仍存在像素级以上偏移，且真实影像对在传感器响应、场景内容、视角、分辨率与拼接缝等多维度差异并非可由简单全局运动模型覆盖；传统方法对模型/超参敏感、失败无报错；学习式匹配对GPU和训练分布依赖强、泛化无保证。

Method: 提出SCDF（Self-Calibrating Displacement Fields）：以稠密逐像素位移场作为运动模型，在金字塔分辨率上执行单次“预测—测量—过滤”循环：利用累计位移场预测移动影像在参考影像中的位置；在预测邻域内用RootSIFT匹配并结合相关性估计亚像素位移；所有阈值通过对该影像对自身的统计自校准；最终得到稠密、稳健的位移场。实现为CPU友好、无训练、无GPU。

Result: 在由Sentinel‑2、Landsat‑8/9与NAIP真实影像构造的584对有真值数据上，相比7个经典基线与2个零样本预训练匹配器，SCDF零失败完成全部配准，将最佳基线的中位端点误差从6.83 m降至4.17 m，90分位误差从17.8 m降至7.77 m；单核CPU可处理8192×8192整景。

Conclusion: SCDF以自校准策略和稠密位移场模型在多源遥感配准中实现稳定、精确、可扩展的无训练方案，显著降低误差并消除失配失败风险，适合大规模、异构场景的运维级应用。

Abstract: Co-registration underlies nearly every multi-temporal and multi-sensor use of optical satellite imagery, and operational products still carry documented offsets well above the fraction-of-a-pixel scale at which change detection, time series, and data fusion degrade. Real image pairs differ along several axes at once (sensor response, scene content, viewing geometry, resolution, mosaic seams), and the last of these is not a single global motion. Existing tools embed a motion model and constants tuned to their development data; a pair that fits is registered precisely, while one that does not either fails to match or returns a result wrong by tens of pixels with no failure reported. Learned matchers add a GPU requirement and carry no accuracy guarantee outside their training distribution. We present SCDF (self-calibrating displacement fields), a training-free, GPU-free estimator whose motion model is the dense per-pixel displacement field itself, so no scene motion falls outside the model. A single predict--measure--filter loop runs over a resolution pyramid: the accumulated field predicts where each patch of the moving image falls in the reference, RootSIFT matching and a correlation pass measure the displacement there to sub-pixel precision, and filters whose thresholds are all calibrated on the image pair itself decide what survives. One configuration, with no per-dataset tuning, processes full $8192^2$ scenes on a single CPU core. On 584 constructed-ground-truth pairs built from real Sentinel-2, Landsat-8/9, and NAIP imagery, against seven classical baselines and two zero-shot pretrained matchers, SCDF registers every pair with zero failures, reduces the best baseline's real-pair median end-point error from 6.83 to 4.17m, and cuts its 90th percentile from 17.8 to 7.77m.

</details>


### [83] [On Tensor-Based PDEs and their Corresponding Variational Formulations with Application to Color Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22302)
*Freddie Åström,George Baravdish,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究探讨哪些PDE可由“数据项+平滑项”的能量泛函导出为Euler-Lagrange方程，并将所得能量用于彩色图像去噪，效果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多图像处理任务（如去噪）可通过最优化能量泛函来求解，若能将给定PDE解释为某个能量的E-L方程，则可获得明确的变分意义、稳定性与可实现的数值方案；但并非所有PDE都对应某个简单的能量形式，需给出判定条件。

Method: 1) 推导PDE成为某能量E[u]=数据保真项+平滑正则项的E-L方程所需的必要条件（如保守场条件、对称性/自伴性、通量为某势的梯度等）。2) 构造满足条件的能量形式，并证明其E-L方程即目标PDE。3) 将该能量用于彩色图像去噪（多通道耦合正则化与数据项设计），实现并与SOTA方法比较。

Result: 提出并证明了PDE可由含数据项与平滑项能量导出的必要条件；基于此得到的彩色图像去噪算法在主流指标（如PSNR/SSIM/色彩保持）和视觉质量上优于多种现有方法。

Conclusion: 给出了PDE变分可表性的判据，并据此构建了有效的彩色图像去噪能量模型；该模型在客观与主观评测上优于现有技术，证明了所提理论与方法的实用性。

Abstract: The case when a partial differential equation (PDE) can be considered as an Euler-Lagrange (E-L) equation of an energy functional, consisting of a data term and a smoothness term is investigated. We show the necessary conditions for a PDE to be the E-L equation for a corresponding functional. This energy functional is applied to a color image denoising problem and it is shown that the method compares favorably to current state-of-the-art color image denoising techniques.

</details>


### [84] [Adapting Dense Vision-Language Relationships for Multi-label Classification with Partial Label](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22313)
*Cheng Chen,Yifan Zhao,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LDSA，用CLIP驱动的语言-视觉适配器解决多标签不完整标注的过拟合与错误先验问题，取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签图像分类常有缺失标注。现有方法依赖统计先验（如共现矩阵、标签依赖）去补全语义，容易不稳定并导致语义错误与过拟合；需要一种能自适应、稳健地从更强的多模态先验中挖掘关系的方法。

Method: 基于CLIP提出Language-driven Dense Semantic Adaptor（LDSA）。(1) 密集对比适配器：在视觉域构建稠密的对比约束，将任务特定知识迁移到视觉特征中，缓解部分标注带来的监督稀疏。(2) 语言驱动交互解码器：结合类别特定的提示学习（prompt tuning），让语言代理与视觉特征交互与适配，捕获标签间隐式关系。二者协同训练，实现先验自适应的语义关系学习。

Result: 在公开多标签分类基准上取得新的SOTA性能；并通过可解释性分析显示模型能发现隐式语义关系且更稳健地处理缺失标注。

Conclusion: 利用CLIP的多模态先验与密集对比/语言交互机制，LDSA在不完整标注下有效缓解统计先验的不稳定与过拟合，实现更强的多标签分类与可解释的、先验自适应的语义关系建模。

Abstract: Learning multi-label image classification with incomplete annotations is a challenging task that has been widely studied for its superior trade-off between high efficiency and less labor consumption on large-scale datasets. Predominant methods rely on strong prior assumptions to recover the missing semantics from partial annotations. However, these statistic priors suffer from unstable semantic mistakes and thus lead to catastrophic overfitting. Toward this end, we propose a Language-driven Dense Semantic Adaptor (LDSA) that excavates prior-adaptive relationships from multimodal pretrained CLIP models. In our approach, the densely contrastive adaptor is first proposed to construct dense visual contrastive constraints, transferring the task-specific knowledge to visual domains. We then propose a language-driven interactive decoder with the help of class-specific prompt tuning, which adapts language proxies with visual domains. With the collaborative learning of proposed modules, experimental results demonstrate our proposed LDSA achieves a new state of the art on public multi-label classification benchmarks, and interpretable analyses reveal that our LDSA discovers implicit semantic relationships with the prior-adaptive learning scheme.

</details>


### [85] [On the Choice of Tensor Estimation for Corner Detection, Optical Flow and Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22314)
*Freddie Åström,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用梯度能量张量替代结构张量处理图像任务，在角点检测、光流与增强中取得相当效果，并在GPU上实现实时增强，帧率提升约40%且画质无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构张量是许多张量型图像方法的基础，但在实时性与实现复杂度上可能受限。作者希望寻找一种在精度不损失的前提下，计算与并行化更友好的替代表示，以提升实际应用（如实时增强）的效率。

Method: 提出并系统性评估“梯度能量张量”作为结构张量的替代：在角点检测、光流估计、迭代式图像增强等任务中，将相关步骤中使用的结构张量替换为梯度能量张量，并在GPU上实现以验证实时性能。

Result: 在多项应用中表现可与结构张量相当；在GPU实现的图像增强任务中，实现实时处理，帧率提升约40%，同时主观与客观画质无明显下降。

Conclusion: 梯度能量张量在若干典型图像任务中可作为结构张量的有效替代，特别适合GPU并行加速，能在保证质量的同时显著提升速度。

Abstract: Many image processing methods such as corner detection, optical flow and iterative enhancement make use of image tensors. Generally, these tensors are estimated using the structure tensor. In this work we show that the gradient energy tensor can be used as an alternative to the structure tensor in several cases. We apply the gradient energy tensor to common image problem applications such as corner detection, optical flow and image enhancement. Our experimental results suggest that the gradient energy tensor enables real-time tensor-based image enhancement using the graphical processing unit (GPU) and we obtain 40% increase of frame rate without loss of image quality.

</details>


### [86] [MedReaMM: Evaluating Large Multimodal Models on Expert-Level Clinical Diagnostic Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22323)
*Lai Wei,Yuchao Chen,Zhenbiao Cao,Xiaojin Zhang,Zhongyu Wei,Bangting Wang,Wei Chen,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedReaMM 提出一个融合病史与多模态影像的诊断综合基准，含625例专家验证个案与ICD-11标注，用于评估模型生成鉴别诊断的能力；对23个LMM评测显示大多准确率<50%，揭示多模态诊断综合存在明显能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多局限于纯文本推理或单一VQA，缺少将临床叙事与多种医学影像整体整合的情境，无法衡量临床专家式的多证据综合诊断能力。

Method: 构建MedReaMM基准：从高水平期刊个案报告与临床病例库收集并筛选稀有/非典型/多系统病例；每例含详细病史与平均2.79幅医学影像；以ICD-11标准化标注共1,042项诊断；在“完全信息”范式下要求模型给出鉴别诊断，并对23个大规模多模态模型进行系统评测与相关性分析。

Result: 多数模型在该基准上的诊断准确率低于50%；诊断表现与医学知识熟练度、医学影像理解、及证据整合能力高度相关。

Conclusion: MedReaMM弥补了多模态医学诊断评测缺口，揭示当前LMM在临床级证据综合上的明显短板，为未来提升医学知识、影像理解与跨模态整合能力指明了改进方向。

Abstract: The application of Large Language Models (LLMs) to diagnostic decision-making has garnered growing interest. However, existing benchmarks largely focus on textual reasoning or isolated visual question-answering (VQA) tasks, lacking holistic integration of clinical narratives and medical imaging, and thus failing to assess the multimodal diagnostic synthesis capability central to expert clinical judgment. To bridge this gap, we introduce MedReaMM, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate models' ability to synthesize heterogeneous clinical evidence consisting of detailed patient histories alongside multiple medical images into accurate differential diagnoses under a complete-information paradigm. Constructed from case reports sourced from top-tier medical journals and curated clinical case databases, MedReaMM comprises 625 expert-validated cases with an average of 2.79 medical images per case and a total of 1,042 standardized diagnoses annotated with ICD-11 codes. These cases predominantly represent rare, atypical, or multi-system presentations that demand expert-level evidence integration beyond routine pattern recognition. We evaluate 23 Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) and find that most achieve diagnostic accuracy scores below 50%, underscoring a substantial gap in multimodal diagnostic synthesis capability. Further analysis reveals that medical knowledge proficiency, medical image understanding, and evidence integration are all highly correlated with diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [87] [AcroMELD: Recovering Interactive PDF Forms with Structure-Aware Graph Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22338)
*Samuel Abramov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AcroMELD，一个将高分辨率视觉Transformer与PDF结构线索融合的表单字段缺失检测器；在内部测试集达0.9344 F1，外部保留集0.8477，但在更严格适配器与稀有类别（签名）上显著掉分，显示域外泛化与长尾问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多看似表单的PDF缺少可交互控件，用户无法直接填写；而检测缺失字段困难，因线索多源且重叠、PDF绘制结构不完全、页面密集且字段众多。需要一种能综合视觉与PDF原语、在复杂页面上稳健发现“应该存在但缺失”的表单控件的方法与评测协议。

Method: 提出AcroMELD：39.4M参数的检测器，融合高分辨率视觉Transformer与无标签PDF原语。使用896个查询：384视觉提案、384结构引导提案、128学习恢复查询；四层graph-set在几何偏置稀疏邻域中交换信息并跨注意到PDF结构；学习同字段关系以链接共指候选；引入定位质量头，按与下游恢复决策一致的“包含感知”重叠训练。定义哈希约束的评测协议，含开发/校准/内部测试/外部隔离保留集。

Result: 在密封单种子设置下：内部测试原生“包含”微F1为0.9344；外部一次性保留集为0.8477（95%引导区间[0.8339,0.8605]），较注册历史基线FFGBT‑v8提升0.0186绝对F1；但在更严格外部适配器下仅获0.7786 IoU‑0.5 F1与0.2900 COCO mAP，且“签名”类别在所选阈值下无预测。

Conclusion: AcroMELD在受控协议和内部/外部基准上总体优于历史参考，满足已注册的运营闸门要求，但在域转移、严格指标与稀有类别上存在明显短板；需改进跨域鲁棒性与长尾类别检测与阈值策略。

Abstract: Interactive PDF form fields are often absent from documents that visually resemble forms, leaving users unable to enter data without printing or external editing tools. Detecting the missing widgets is difficult because a field may be indicated by several overlapping cues, born-digital PDFs expose useful but incomplete drawing structure, and dense pages can contain hundreds of fields. We introduce AcroMELD (AcroForm Multi-source Evidence Linking Decoder), a 39.4M-parameter detector that combines a high-resolution visual transformer with label-free PDF primitives. Its 896-query set comprises 384 visual proposals, 384 structure-seeded proposals, and 128 learned recovery queries. Four graph-set layers exchange information over geometry-biased sparse neighborhoods and cross-attend to PDF structure. A learned same-field relation links co-referent candidates, while a localization-quality head is trained on the containment-aware overlap used by the downstream recovery decision. We define a hash-bound evaluation protocol with disjoint development, calibration, internal-test, and quarantined external-holdout roles. The sealed, single-seed candidate reaches native containment micro-$F_1$ 0.9344 on the internal test and 0.8477 on the one-shot external holdout (95% PDF-cluster bootstrap interval [0.8339, 0.8605]). This passes the registered historical FFGBT-v8 reference by 0.0186 absolute $F_1$. Under the stricter external adapter, however, performance is 0.7786 IoU-$0.5$ $F_1$ and 0.2900 COCO mAP, below a locally evaluated CommonForms-L reference; the signature class receives no prediction at the selected threshold. Thus the result supports the registered operational gate while exposing substantial domain and rare-class limitations.

</details>


### [88] [TransHands: Repurposing Human Pose Encoders as Hand Pose Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22341)
*Milo Piccioli,Gianluca Amprimo,Claudia Ferraris,Gabriella Olmo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TransHands提出将大规模人体动作表示迁移到手部领域，通过两阶段训练+轻量输入适配模块，把2D手部关键点映射到预训练人体运动编码器的表示空间，在多种架构上稳定提升3D手势估计精度并增强跨域泛化，尤其在自我视角场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部3D姿态从单目2D提升困难，主要因缺乏大规模多样的带3D标注手部数据；而人体动作数据丰富。希望复用人体动作先验，避免从零训练手部特定生物力学模型。

Method: 提出TransHands：骨干无关的迁移学习框架。用已在人体姿态语料上预训练的运动编码器作为核心，通过：1）两阶段训练与微调策略；2）轻量级手部输入适配模块，使手部运动学与全身运动表示空间对齐。输入为2D手部姿态，输出3D手部姿态。框架可接入Transformer、图模型、频域模型等多种架构。

Result: 在四种SOTA运动建模架构上验证，人体动作先验可稳定迁移：带来一致的精度提升、对跨域特别是自我视角场景有更强泛化，并可用于真实世界下游任务。

Conclusion: 通过对齐表示空间与分阶段适配，TransHands有效将人体动作先验移植到手部3D估计，架构无关、数据效率更高、泛化更强，适合实际应用场景。

Abstract: Lifting 3D hand poses from 2D monocular representations remains challenging due to the limited availability of large-scale, diverse 3D-annotated hand datasets, in contrast to the abundance of human body motion data. We address this limitation by transferring motion representations learned from large body pose corpora to the hand domain. We introduce TransHands, a backbone-agnostic transfer learning framework that enables pre-trained human motion encoders to be effectively adapted for 3D hand pose estimation from 2D pose inputs. Rather than training hand-specific biomechanical models from scratch, TransHands combines a two-stage training and fine-tuning strategy with a lightweight hand-specific input adaptation module that aligns hand kinematics with the representation space learned for full-body motion. We evaluate TransHands across four state-of-the-art motion modeling architectures, including transformer-based, graph-based, and frequency- domain models. Results demonstrate that motion priors learned from body pose data transfer consistently across architectures, yielding consistent accuracy gains, strong cross-domain generalization, particularly in challenging egocentric settings, and applicability for downstream tasks in real-world contexts.

</details>


### [89] [Fast and Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting with Polarized Opacity Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22344)
*Zi-Ming Wang,Kai-Wen Duan,Kowei Huang,Akihiro Sugimoto,Shang-Hong Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种针对3D Gaussian Splatting(3DGS)的高效训练框架，避免“先增密再剪枝”的模式膨胀问题，通过L2重建损失与极化不透明先验(POP)协同，使有用高斯趋向全不透明、无用高斯趋向透明，实现天然剪枝与更快渲染，在多数据集上以更少高斯保持相当画质并加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS依赖“增密—剪枝”循环：为覆盖误差先大量生成高斯，再靠剪枝压缩。这会产生大量低不透明度且冗余的高斯，导致模型臃肿、内存与训练成本偏高。需要一种在训练过程中本身就保持紧凑表示的方法，以减少冗余同时维持重建质量与速度。

Method: 提出内在紧凑的训练框架：1) 使用L2重建损失，提供与误差成比例的稳定梯度以改善优化；2) 引入Polarized Opacity Prior (POP)：对高斯的不透明度施加先验，使“信息性”高斯被驱动到高不透明，非信息性高斯被驱动到透明，从而在训练中自动管理高斯数量；3) 借助POP带来的透明化促进自然剪枝，并利用Early Ray Termination加速渲染。整体上替代传统的“增密—剪枝”循环。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，方法以显著更少的高斯数量实现与SOTA 3DGS可比的可视化重建质量，同时加速训练与渲染（因早停与更小模型），体现出稳定、快速且紧凑的3D表示学习。

Conclusion: 通过L2重建与POP的协同，可在不依赖激进增密与事后剪枝的前提下，获得本质紧凑的3DGS模型，减少内存与训练成本且维持画质，提供了一条简单有效的快速、紧凑3DGS训练途径。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality at real-time speeds but suffers from "model bloat" - a large number of redundant, low-opacity Gaussians that inflate memory usage and training costs. This inefficiency stems from the standard "densify-then-prune" paradigm, which expands the model aggressively before relying on pruning to achieve compactness. To mitigate this problem, we present an efficient training framework that builds an intrinsically compact representation, replacing the conventional densify-then-prune cycle. Our method leverages a synergistic design: an L2 reconstruction loss to provide error-proportional gradients that stabilize optimization, and a novel Polarized Opacity Prior (POP) to actively manage the Gaussian population. POP steers informative primitives toward full opacity and uninformative ones toward transparency, enabling natural pruning and accelerating rendering through Early Ray Termination. Experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently achieves accelerated 3DGS training with significantly fewer Gaussians while maintaining comparable visual reconstruction quality. These results show that the proposed framework provides a simple and effective path toward fast and inherently compact 3DGS training.

</details>


### [90] [Multimodal examination answer data with expert-designed Outcome-Based Education rubrics for criterion-level assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22346)
*Jahangir Alam SM,Md Khalid Syfullah,Saad Ahmed,Munira Akter Mou,A K Z Rasel Rahman,A. K. M. Masudur Rahman,Mohammed Sowket Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发布了一个多模态、带OBE评分元数据的扫描答卷数据集：含485份答卷、12种评分量表、47个细则，配套题目、标准答案、评分标准与总分校验，可用于自动评分与多模态文档理解等研究，研究用途受限获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评分/多模态文档理解缺少高质量、细粒度、真实场景的带评分依据的数据集，尤其是能反映OBE（成果导向教育）理念、包含手写与多种扫描噪声、并提供细则级（criterion-level）标注与可核验分数的资源。

Method: 构建与整理数据集：从4所高校、8位教师收集9门课、12类题型的考试材料与评分量表；扫描获得含手写、公式、代码、图表等的PDF答卷，来源含手机与台式扫描以引入多样成像变化；为每份答卷关联随机ID、学科/题目/标准答案、评分细则与等级描述、细则分与总分；执行异源整合、文本与标签标准化、分数校验、ID/文件名随机化、JSON-PDF一致性检查，并做逐答卷审计确保唯一性与分数一致。

Result: 得到含485份独立答卷、12套评分量表共47个评分细则的多模态数据集；每份答卷的总分与细则分求和一致且不超rubric上限；文件与ID均唯一；扫描质量与书写风格高度多样，便于鲁棒性与泛化研究。

Conclusion: 该数据集为基于量表的自动评测、细则级反馈、分数预测与多模态理解提供真实且可核验的基准，但出于隐私与伦理考虑仅限科研用途，需向通讯作者合理申请获取。

Abstract: This data article describes a multimodal collection of scanned examination answers paired with expert-designed Outcome-Based Education (OBE) grading metadata. The collection contains 485 answer submissions from 415 consenting students at four academic institutions. Eight faculty contributors supplied examination materials covering nine subjects and 12 distinct question templates. Each answer-level item links a scanned PDF to a randomized identifier, subject label, question, model answer, criterion definitions, performance-level descriptions, criterion marks, and a total mark. The 12 rubrics contain 47 criteria in total. The scans retain realistic academic content, including handwriting, printed text, equations, tables, code, figures, sketches, and diagrams. CamScanner, Adobe Scan, and conventional scanners contributed variation in illumination, contrast, orientation, compression, and resolution. Diverse handwriting, crossed-out work, revised calculations, and inserted corrections add further visual variability for robustness and generalization studies. Preparation involved heterogeneous-source consolidation, label and text standardization, score validation, identifier randomization, filename randomization, and JSON-to-PDF integrity checks. An answer-level audit confirmed 485 unique identifiers, 485 unique PDF filenames, agreement between each total mark and its criterion-mark sum, and scores within the applicable rubric maximum. The data can support rubric-aware automated evaluation, multimodal document understanding, criterion-level feedback, score prediction, and privacy-aware OBE assessment research. Access is restricted to research use and is available from the corresponding author upon reasonable request.

</details>


### [91] [Pre-Decoding Acoustic Triage for Budgeted Vision-Language Captioning of Untrimmed Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22359)
*Masoud Jalayer,Changyi Li,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“音频优先分流（audio-first triage）”以在长时 egocentric 视频分析中减少昂贵的VLM调用，通过先用极轻量的音频特征筛选片段，再对入选窗口调用VLM，在不解码任何视频帧前完成筛选；以“每个动作触发一次”为目标训练选择器，显著提升动作覆盖率并减少调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对小时级自摄视频的进度监测、质检与安全需要自动分析，但主流做法在固定短窗上频繁调用VLM，成本随调用次数线性增长，难以承受。已有分流策略要么均匀采样（覆盖差），要么依赖视觉特征排序（需先视频解码，违背省算初衷）。

Method: 提出“音频优先分流”：在任何视频帧解码前，基于轻量的音频模态对时间窗口打分并选择VLM调用窗口；使用冻结的AudioSet预训练特征，无需领域声音事件标签；关键创新是训练目标从逐帧事件检测转为“每个动作触发一次”的触发策略，以提高动作级覆盖；该分流可以与token压缩/量化自然组合。

Result: 在所有评估的调用率下，动作覆盖率提升4.0-10.8个百分点；在EK-100以不到一半可用调用数达成匹配覆盖时，进一步减少9-20%的VLM调用；在Ego4D（247段）中，中等调用率范围优于均匀采样；且超过两种近期视觉关键帧选择器。

Conclusion: 音频先行、按动作触发的分流在无需视频解码与领域标签的前提下，显著提升动作覆盖并降低VLM调用成本，实用且可与下游压缩方法兼容；代码与结果已开放以复现。

Abstract: Automatically analyzing hours-long egocentric video is increasingly essential for progress monitoring, quality control, and safety in logistics, construction, and manufacturing. Yet current pipelines that process short, fixed-size windows with a vision-language model (VLM) are prohibitively expensive because cost scales with the number of model calls. To reduce this cost, prior work proposes triage policies to select which windows merit a VLM invocation. However, these policies either sample uniformly or rank windows using visual features, which ironically requires the video decoding that the budget constraints are meant to avoid. We propose audio-first triage: select windows using the lightest modality, scored before any video frame is decoded, so the approach composes naturally with token compression or quantization. The novelty lies in the objective, not the representation: rather than a per-frame sound-event detector, we train the selector to trigger once per action. This objective shift improves action coverage by 4.0-10.8 percentage points across all evaluated call rates, using frozen AudioSet-pretrained features without domain-specific sound-event labels. Using fewer than half of the available calls, the triage cuts 9-20% of VLM calls at matched coverage on EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (EK-100), surpasses uniform sampling through the mid-range on Ego4D over 247 clips, and outperforms two recent visual keyframe selectors. Code, the reference implementation and every results file this manuscript reads are at https://github.com/masjalayer/PreDecoding-AcousticTriage.

</details>


### [92] [When Do VLMs Help Arabic Manuscript OCR? A Cross-Dataset Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22366)
*Moshiur Farazi,Firoj Alam,Abderrahmane Maaradji,Zakaria Maamar,Hamdy Mubarak,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估传统OCR、通用/阿语专用VLM及OCR条件化VLM在8个阿语数据集上的识别效果：无单一方法通吃；VLM在页级手稿优于Tesseract，行级接近；在多场景下，基于OCR先验的VLM纠错可胜过单独OCR或VLM。提出“OCR先验可回收性”原则：当OCR输出在视觉与文本上仍可被图像与VLM共同恢复时，条件化有益；若先验错配或系统性误导则有害。对重音符、预处理、生成预算与重复环敏感，建议基于脚本与可回收性等路由的自适应OCR-VLM流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿语与伊斯兰手稿识别长期受限，VLM在文档理解方面崭露头角但在该领域应用不足。需要系统比较传统OCR与VLM（含阿语专用与OCR条件化）在多域阿语文档上的表现，揭示何时、为何、如何使用它们。

Method: 覆盖8个阿语数据集（历史手稿、老旧印刷、清洁印刷、多域文档、手写等），比较：1）传统OCR（如Tesseract）；2）通用VLM；3）阿语专用VLM；4）OCR输出作为条件输入的VLM纠错。评估行级与页级，并做消融与诊断（对变音符、预处理、生成步数/预算、重复循环的敏感性）。

Result: 不存在一统方案。行级历史手稿：VLM接近Tesseract；页级手稿：VLM更好。多设置下，OCR条件化纠错优于单独OCR与单独VLM。在老旧/清洁印刷、混合域阿语与部分纳斯赫（Naskh）手稿上有提升；在马格里比（Maghribi）手稿与真实学生手写上因脚本错配或系统误导而变差。

Conclusion: 提出“OCR先验可回收性”原则：当OCR输出仍提供与图像对齐的锚点时，VLM可据此细化；若先验错配则伤害性能。应采用自适应OCR-VLM工作流：按脚本类型、先验可回收性、长度/失败指示器进行路由与选择，并关注重音符、预处理、生成预算和重复问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being used for document understanding, yet their role in Arabic and Islamic manuscript recognition remains underexplored. To address such a gap in this paper, we evaluate traditional OCR, general-purpose VLMs, Arabic-specialized VLMs, and OCR-conditioned VLM correction across eight Arabic text datasets spanning historical manuscripts, aged printed books, clean print, multi-domain documents, and handwriting. The results show that no single approach dominates across setups. On line-level historical manuscripts, VLMs are close to Tesseract; on page-level manuscript images, they perform better; and in several settings, an OCR-conditioned corrector improves over both standalone OCR and standalone VLMs. The central finding is an OCR-prior recoverability principle: OCR conditioning helps when the OCR output remains visually and textually recoverable, providing anchors that the VLM can refine against the image. It improves recognition on aged print, clean print, mixed-domain Arabic, and some Naskh manuscripts, but degrades performance when the prior is script-mismatched or systematically misleading, as in Maghribi manuscripts and realistic student handwriting. Additional diagnostics show that Arabic VLM-OCR is sensitive to diacritics, preprocessing, generation budget, and repetition loops. These findings support an adaptive OCR-VLM workflow that routes pages according to script, OCR-prior recoverability, length diagnostics, and failure-mode indicators.

</details>


### [93] [DiD It in 87 Minutes: A Label-Free Softmax-to-Linear Adaptation of Vision Transformers for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22368)
*Huaiyuan Qin,Gabriel James Goenawan,Zihang Lin,Muli Yang,Hongyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Detector-Interface Distillation（DiD），把已训练的Softmax注意力ViT检测器骨干无标签转为线性注意力版本，通过对齐“检测器接口”张量，显著缩短推理时延与内存且性能接近监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力在高分辨率检测中计算/显存优势明显，但直接将已训练的Softmax注意力骨干替换为线性注意力会显著掉点；通用的无标签蒸馏在检测任务上往往失效。根因在于检测器下游头部固定，需保持其期望的特征接口一致。

Method: 冻结Softmax教师的检测器整体或其下游组件，仅训练线性注意力骨干；不以分类标签或中间隐状态为目标，而是对齐“面向检测器的接口张量”（即下游头部期望的特征输出），实现无标签蒸馏。

Result: 在DOTA-v1.5上，DiD显著优于既有基线，并可匹配从头监督训练的线性模型；适配约87分钟（4GPU）；推理延迟降低约62%，峰值显存降低约49%。

Conclusion: DiD提供了一种简单的、无标签的路径，将已训练的Softmax检测器转为高效的线性注意力版本，并提示在架构转换中应关注“接口感知”的目标对齐。

Abstract: While linear attention is a compelling mechanism for high-resolution object detection due to its reduced cost for global token mixing, converting the Softmax-attention ViT backbone of a trained detector into a linear-attention one is not a trivial drop-in replacement. Directly swapping the attention operator leads to severe performance degradation, and generic label-free distillation, though effective for classification, often fails on detection tasks. We argue that the central challenge is \textit{detector-interface preservation}: the converted backbone must reproduce the exact feature tensors expected by the fixed downstream detector, rather than merely imitating internal Softmax hidden states. To address this, we introduce Detector-Interface Distillation (DiD), a label-free conversion method that exclusively trains the linear-attention backbone by aligning detector-facing interface tensors with those of a frozen Softmax teacher. On DOTA-v1.5, DiD substantially outperforms established baselines and matches supervised, fully trained linear models. Adaptation completes in roughly 87 minutes on 4 GPUs, and the linearized backbone cuts inference latency by ~62% and peak memory by ~49%. We hope our findings offer the community a simple, label-free route to reusing trained Softmax detectors as efficient linear ones, and encourage interface-aware objectives in future architecture-conversion work.

</details>


### [94] [LiST: Local-Simplex Test-Time LoRA Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22370)
*Yihua Shao,Jia Li,Siyu Chen,Xinyu Luo,Yang Liu,Kecheng Chen,Xinwei Long,Lingyu Zhu,Fanhu Zeng,Maolin Wang,Ziyang Yan,Jingcai Guo,Hao Tang,Nicu Sebe,Zhenyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiST：在测试时对LoRA进行无标签自适应融合，按样本动态搜索融合权重，优于静态合并与常规测试时自适应，并提升对未见任务的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA适配器为不同任务提供模块化微调，但组合方式多为静态，无法针对具体测试样本自适应，且可能破坏任务特异性与泛化能力。需要一种在不改动主干与适配器权重的前提下，按样本动态选择与融合适配器的方法。

Method: 构建LiST：1）用LoRA参数锚点与提示级行为向量形成联合任务表征；2）基于目标条件检索邻近适配器，定义局部单纯形作为搜索空间；3）进行“保枝”式融合，仅在局部分支内线性组合；4）用包含先验、几何与随机一致性约束的提示级能量函数筛选候选权重；5）通过安全接受规则决定是否采用候选，否则回退到目标条件先验。全过程不更新主干或适配器参数。

Result: 在多模态与纯语言基准上，LiST较静态LoRA合并与传统测试时自适应基线取得更高性能；同时保持各任务适配器效用，并在未见任务上展现更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 测试时、无标签、样本自适应的LoRA融合可在不改动模型参数的情况下有效提升性能与泛化；将适配器库转化为目标条件的局部单纯形并配合安全选择机制，是比静态合并更稳健实用的方案。

Abstract: Task-specific LoRA adapters offer a modular way to specialize large language and vision-language models. However, existing adapter composition methods are mostly static and cannot adapt to individual test inputs. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{LiST}, a label-free test-time LoRA fusion framework that converts an existing LoRA bank into a target-conditioned local simplex and searches sample-specific fusion weights at inference time. LiST builds joint task representations from LoRA parameter anchors and prompt-level behavior vectors, retrieves neighboring adapters as a local search space, and performs branch-preserving fusion without updating the backbone or adapters. Candidate weights are selected by a prompt-level energy with prior, geometric, and stochastic-consistency constraints, and are deployed only when they pass a safe acceptance rule. Otherwise, LiST falls back to a target-conditioned prior. Experiments on multimodal and language benchmarks show that LiST outperforms static LoRA merging and conventional test-time adaptation baselines, while preserving task-specific adapter utility and improving robustness on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [95] [M$^3$ISR: A Multi-Modal Multi-View Benchmark for 3D/4D Gaussian Splatting and Feedforward Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22465)
*Xinhui Liu,Lei Liu,Zhenghao Chen,Lebin Zhou,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M^3ISR：一个可控的合成基准，用于系统评测3D/4D 高斯点渲染在重建、压缩与流式传输中的质量-效率权衡。含25个多视角场景、完整标注与五条赛道，突出控制相机几何与时间冗余以公平比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FVV/交互渲染越来越用显式高斯表示，但在规模、动态更新与算力成本上存在部署瓶颈；现有真实多视视频基准难以分离相机几何、表示效率与时间冗余等因素，阻碍方法的可控、公平对比。

Method: 构建可控合成数据集M^3ISR：五类室内外场景、两种相机/运动配置、六路同步1080p视角、共享中心的相机设计，以隔离角度变化；提供密集真值（RGB、相机参数、深度、语义/实例分割、静动态掩码）；组织五条评测赛道：3DGS合成、4DGS合成、4DGS流式、3DGS压缩、4DGS压缩；定义前向（feedforward）压缩任务与参考率失真公式，给出基线结果。

Result: 在静态重建质量上，各方法差异较小；在表示存储开销上差异显著。已评测的流式方法相比离线动态重建基线报告更高的训练或重建成本。提供3DGS/4DGS前向压缩的初步基线与RD评估。

Conclusion: M^3ISR为高斯表征的FVV重建、压缩与流式传输提供一个可控、互补的测试平台，可系统研究质量、存储与计算开销间的权衡，并促进方法在受控几何与时间冗余条件下的公平比较。

Abstract: High-fidelity free-viewpoint video (FVV) and interactive rendering increasingly rely on explicit Gaussian representations, yet practical deployment remains constrained by representation size, dynamic updates, and computational cost. Existing multi-view video benchmarks provide valuable real-captured content, but they make it difficult to isolate the effects of controlled camera geometry, representation efficiency, and temporal redundancy. We introduce M$^3$ISR, a controlled synthetic benchmark for 3D and 4D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS/4DGS). The benchmark contains 25 scenes from five indoor and outdoor scene groups, two camera/motion configurations, six synchronized 1080p views, and dense ground-truth annotations including RGB, camera parameters, depth, semantic and instance segmentation, and static--dynamic masks. The shared-center camera design intentionally isolates angular view variation and enables controlled evaluation of novel-view synthesis and representation efficiency. We organize M$^3$ISR into five complementary tracks covering 3DGS synthesis, 4DGS synthesis, 4DGS streaming, 3DGS compression, and 4DGS compression. Representative baseline results show small differences in static reconstruction quality but substantial differences in representation storage, while the evaluated streaming methods exhibit substantially higher reported training or reconstruction cost than the corresponding offline dynamic reconstruction baselines. We further define feedforward compression tasks for 3DGS and 4DGS and provide reference rate--distortion formulations and preliminary baseline evaluations. The benchmark is intended as a controlled and complementary testbed for systematic study of Gaussian-based FVV reconstruction, compression, and streaming.

</details>


### [96] [HeatTok: Enhancing Remote Sensing Image Understanding via Thermodiffusion-based Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22485)
*Yingying Yan,Jiaqi Tang,Wei Wei,Qianzhou Wang,Jinjian Wu,Botong Geng,Jianmin Chen,Yuyang Xia,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeatTok提出一种面向遥感图像的语义感知可变形可视化分词器，通过“热扩散”式区域聚合生成与物体边界对齐的不规则token，并引入G-MRoPE以高斯方式编码中心、尺度和朝向，显著缓解补丁分块导致的语义混叠与目标破碎，在VRSBench与EarthVQA上以合理token预算达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM视觉分词多采用固定网格patch，将不规则地物切碎并混入背景，导致遥感图理解中的目标级语义被破坏。已有自适应方法难以精确抽取对象级token，且缺乏针对不规则区域的几何位置编码，限制了模型对真实地物形状与空间分布的感知。

Method: 1) 热扩散聚合：受热传导启发，依据区域相似性与边界阻抗自适应合并相邻同质区域，形成语义独立、与对象轮廓对齐的不规则token（HeatTok）。2) 几何位置编码：提出Gaussian Multimodal RoPE（G-MRoPE），用2D高斯刻画token空间分布，显式注入中心、尺度、方向等几何先验，使MLLM能感知不规则形状。3) 在VRSBench、EarthVQA上以控制token预算进行评估与对比。

Result: HeatTok在对象级语义完整性保持方面显著优于基线，在VRSBench和EarthVQA上取得SOTA；在相同或更少token数下达到更高准确率/检索与问答性能，显示更优的效率—精度权衡。

Conclusion: 面向遥感图像的热扩散驱动语义分词与高斯几何位置编码可有效生成对象对齐的不规则token并提升MLLM性能；方法在合理token预算下达SOTA，验证了对象级token与几何先验对不规则场景理解的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Current visual tokenizers in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on patch-based partitioning, which causes severe semantic mixture and object fragmentation in remote sensing imagery due to the irregular contours of geo-objects. Moreover, existing adaptive methods struggle to extract precise object-level tokens and lack dedicated geometric positional encodings for irregular regions. In this paper, we propose HeatTok, a semantic-aware tokenizer driven by thermodiffusion aggregation. Inspired by the physical principles of heat conduction, HeatTok adaptively merges adjacent homogeneous regions to generate semantically independent, object-aligned irregular tokens. To enable MLLMs to perceive these irregular shapes, we design the Gaussian Multimodal Rotary Positional Embedding (G-MRoPE), which models token spatial distributions via 2D Gaussians and explicitly injects center, scale, and orientation cues. Extensive evaluations on the VRSBench and EarthVQA datasets demonstrate that HeatTok effectively preserves object-level semantic integrity and achieves state-of-the-art performance under a reasonable token budget. The code is available: https://github.com/YingyingYan1/HeatTok.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Sample-wise Rank-aware Interpolation Weights for Composed Visual Data Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22500)
*Boseung Jeong,Taegyu Park,Donghyeon Kwon,Hyunsouk Cho,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SRAlN：以动态权重线性插值替代MLLM融合，实现组合视觉检索（参考视觉+文本修改）低延迟与SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM用于融合带来高查询时延与扩展性差；线性插值虽快但固定权重难以兼顾与目标接近和与负样本可判别性。

Method: 在嵌入空间内进行参考视觉与文本修改向量的线性插值，并由模型动态预测“查询特定”的插值权重；训练期引入批级、排序感知（rank-aware）的权重估计以绕过显式最优权重标注；推理期使用紧凑记忆库合成“困难负样本”，以更好地评估并选择权重；整体无需MLLM，简化计算。

Result: 在组合视频检索上取得最佳性能，在组合图像检索上与现SOTA持平；同时显著降低相较MLLM方案的查询时延。

Conclusion: 简单、可扩展的动态线性插值在组合检索中足以替代复杂MLLM融合；通过排序感知训练与记忆库困难负样本，使权重学习可行并提升判别与检索效率。

Abstract: At the heart of composed visual data retrieval is the fusion of a reference visual input and a textual modification into a single query. While current state-of-the-art methods utilize multimodal large language models for this fusion, their complexity introduces prohibitive querytime latency, limiting their scalability. We instead revisit the efficacy of simple linear interpolation within an embedding space, and introduce SRAIN, the first framework that dynamically predicts query-specific interpolation weights. The key challenge lies in the fact that the quality of an interpolation weight should be measured by the interpolated embedding's discriminability from negatives as well as its proximity to true targets; this makes collecting and predicting optimal weights intractable. We overcome this bottleneck through two key innovations: batch-wise rank-aware weight estimation during training, and a compact memory bank that synthesizes hard negatives during inference. SRAIN achieves the best in composed video retrieval and matches the current state of the art in composed image retrieval, all while substantially reducing querytime latency compared to MLLM-based alternatives.

</details>


### [98] [TRACE: Temporal Retrieval with Anchored and Convergent Evidence for Long-Horizon Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22516)
*Pengyiang Liu,Junbo Niu,Xiaoyang Hu,Zhongyue Shi,Zitian Wang,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VES-Bench用于审计长视频问答中的“证据覆盖”，并引入无需训练的TRACE代理，循环收集原始片段证据直至答案稳定，在同一骨干下以更少帧数实现更高或相近的正确率，并在多基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答里，模型常被只看最终答案或粗粒度证据区间来评估，缺乏对“实际解码并观察到的帧”的审计，导致即使答对也可能基于不完整观察。需要一个基准能严格检查答案所依赖的每个事件是否被实际解码覆盖，并探索可在有限帧预算下可靠找齐证据的方法。

Method: 1) 构建VES-Bench：包含348个公开长视频、600道题（时间顺序与事件计数），为每题标注一组“共同必要”的证据区间；设计三种严格度的审计，检查方法解码的帧是否落入每个证据区间。2) 提出TRACE：训练-free代理，逐轮从原始视觉片段中抽取并累积“证据包”，每轮基于当前证据回答；当答案在证据包扩充后保持稳定，且对同一片段做最终复核仍给出相同答案时停止，以此确保证据充分性与答案稳定性。3) 同骨干审计并与均匀采样基线在帧预算和准确率上比较。

Result: 在同一骨干下，TRACE在“每个证据区间至少命中两帧”的严格审计条件下，达到50.7%正确率，平均98.7帧/题；优于128帧均匀解码的40.2%，并以0.39倍帧成本接近256帧均匀解码（相差2.6点）。同时在该审计中获得最高最终答案准确率63.5%。在Video-MME得分86.1、LVBench 75.6、LongVideoBench 75.1，表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: VES-Bench提供了可审计“证据覆盖”的评测视角；TRACE无需训练即可通过循证、稳定性停止准则，在更低帧预算下实现更好的证据完整性与答案准确率，并在多基准保持竞争力。

Abstract: A long-video answer is evidence-supported only when the frames decoded from the video cover every event the answer depends on. Existing evaluations score final-answer correctness or predicted evidence intervals, but the frames a method decodes before answering are rarely audited, so correct answers can still rest on incomplete observation. We introduce VES-Bench, a 600-question benchmark of Temporal Ordering and Event Counting items over 348 public long videos. Each item carries a jointly necessary set of evidence intervals, letting us audit at three strictness levels whether a method's decoded frames cover every one of them. We also propose TRACE, a training-free agent that grounds answers in raw visual clips, builds an evidence bundle round by round, and stops only when the answer stabilises as the bundle grows and a final pass over the same clips returns the same answer. Under a same-backbone audit, TRACE answers 50.7% of questions correctly with at least two decoded frames inside every evidence interval, at 98.7 frames per question: over 10 points above uniform decoding at 128 frames (40.2%), and within 2.6 points of uniform decoding at 256 frames at 0.39x its frame cost, while reaching the highest answer accuracy in the audit (63.5%). TRACE also stays competitive on Video-MME (86.1), LVBench (75.6), and LongVideoBench (75.1).

</details>


### [99] [VISTA: Test-Time Compositional Alignment for Visual Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22521)
*Hossein Shahabadi,Niki Sepasian,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISTA：首个用于视觉自回归（VAR）文本到图像生成的梯度式测试时对齐框架，可在不改动参数的前提下提升合成约束遵循度（属性绑定、空间关系），显著提高目标构成指标且保持/提升主观质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR在速度与质量上具备相对扩散模型的优势，但同样存在组合性失败（属性绑定、空间关系错误）。扩散已有丰富的测试时对齐方法，而VAR的离散、多分辨率、带状态的采样特性使现有方法不可直接套用，形成方法学空缺。

Method: 基于Infinity骨干，在生成过程中对中间表示进行可微优化，通过冻结的Transformer和可扩展的跨注意力可微约束目标，直接干预多尺度自回归采样；引入跨尺度稳定优化机制，无需微调或额外训练。

Result: 在两套基准与两种模型规模上，所有目标组合类别均提升：2B模型平均目标分数提升约20%，8B模型提升约6%，空间关系提升最大；图像质量不降反升，独立偏好模型（非优化目标）偏好率提升近20%；2B+VISTA超过原生8B骨干。

Conclusion: 测试时对齐可大幅弥合VAR模型规模带来的组合性差距；VISTA在不更改参数的前提下稳定、通用、可扩展，能在保持质量的同时显著提升组合遵循度，尤其是空间关系。

Abstract: Visual autoregressive (VAR) models have emerged as a fast, high-quality alternative to diffusion for text-to-image generation, but like diffusion models they exhibit persistent compositional failures, producing images that violate the attribute bindings and spatial relations specified in the prompt. While a rich line of test-time alignment methods has developed for diffusion, no comparable approach exists for next-scale VAR generation, whose stateful, discrete, multi-resolution sampling process makes existing techniques inapplicable. We close this gap with \textbf{VISTA} (\textbf{Vi}sual Autoregressive \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{T}est-time \textbf{A}lignment), the first gradient-based test-time alignment framework for next-scale autoregressive image generation. Built on Infinity, VISTA intervenes directly in the generation process, optimizing intermediate representations through the frozen transformer to steer visual predictions toward compositional constraints, without modifying model parameters or requiring additional training. VISTA introduces the mechanisms needed to make such optimization stable across scales, together with an extensible objective space that any differentiable constraint on cross-attention can plug into. Across two benchmarks and two model scales, VISTA improves every targeted compositional category, raising the mean targeted score by nearly 20\% on a 2B backbone and almost 6\% on an 8B backbone, with the largest gains on spatial relations. Image quality is preserved: an independent preference model VISTA never optimizes scores its outputs nearly 20\% higher. Notably, the 2B model with VISTA surpasses a backbone four times its size, indicating that a substantial part of the compositional gap between model scales is recoverable at test time.

</details>


### [100] [RS$^3$-Prune: Read-Sparse, Store-Sparse Token Pruning for Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22526)
*Avilasha Mandal,Sarvesh Shashikumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RS^3-Prune，一种无需再训练、在推理时通过少量钩子即可对基于记忆库的VOS模型进行token剪枝的方法，显著降低延时与峰值显存而几乎不损精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VOS依赖密集图像token并把历帧token累积进记忆库，视频越长，token预算越大，导致每帧延时和峰值显存暴涨，难以支持长视频或受限设备的实时部署；需要在不改动训练的前提下压缩这类模型的内存与计算。

Method: 在两处进行训练无关的token剪枝：1) 编码器输出与内存注意力读出之间，按几何先验只保留少量查询token参与跨帧注意力；2) 记忆编码器与记忆库之间，仅允许处于目标空间范围内的token进入记忆库，从源头控制记忆体量。该方案作为推理时hooks嵌入任意记忆库式VOS管线。

Result: 在多项VOS基准上实现最高38.8%的FPS提升与13.1%的峰值显存降低，同时维持与原模型竞争的J&F指标。

Conclusion: 直接在token预算维度做压缩可显著提速降耗而保持精度；RS^3-Prune为记忆库式VOS提供通用、训练无关、易集成的推理加速与内存削减方案。

Abstract: We introduce RS$^3$-Prune, a training-free token-pruning recipe that instantiates as a small set of inference time hooks atop existing video object segmentation (VOS) networks. Modern VOS models have converged on a common, expensive design: an image encoder produces a dense token grid for every frame, and a memory bank accumulates these tokens across all previously processed frames to condition future predictions. As a video grows longer, the resulting token budget governs both per-frame latency and peak GPU memory. Hence these models break on use cases such as --- long-form video or real-time deployment on memory-bounded accelerators. In this work we argue that the right axis along which to compress memory-bank VOS is the token budget itself. RS$^3$-Prune operates in two precise locations within an arbitrary memory-bank VOS pipeline: at the boundary between the image encoder and the memory-attention readout, where we restrict the queries that participate in the cross-frame attention to only a small, geometrically informed subset; and at the boundary between the memory encoder and the memory bank, where we restrict which tokens are ever permitted to enter the bank to those that lie within the object's spatial extent. Over various established benchmarks, RS$^3$-Prune delivers up to $38.8\%$ FPS speedup and reduces $13.1\%$ peak memory usage, while preserving a competitive $\mathcal{J}$&$\mathcal{F}$ compared to the unmodified VOS networks.

</details>


### [101] [SymmAdapt: Symmetrical Flow Matching for Source-Free Domain Adaptation in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22532)
*Tal Grossman,Noa Cahan,Hayit Greenspan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对称流匹配（Symmetrical Flow Matching, SFM）的源数据不可用的无监督域自适应（SFUDA）分割框架：同一流同时实现从图像到掩码的分割与从掩码到“源风格”图像的合成，并以高斯起点确保跨域结构一致。利用对称性在目标无标注数据上生成可靠伪标签与源样式合成图，构建生成回放缓冲，在生成式自训练中与真实目标图像联合微调，从而在多器官腹部、心脏（MRI↔CT）及多站点前列腺分割上优于SFUDA基线，接近传统UDA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗影像分割在跨模态（如MRI与CT）及跨站点存在显著域移，限制临床落地。SFUDA希望在无法访问源域隐私数据时，仅用无标注目标域适配预训练模型，但现有方法常受纹理统计漂移影响、伪标签不稳、灾难性遗忘。作者动机是：用一个统一的生成-分割模型，从域无关的起点出发，借助结构先验保持解剖一致，并在无源数据条件下用生成回放稳固源知识。

Method: 构建SFM统一生成模型：同一学习到的流既可从输入图像推至掩码实现分割，也可从掩码生成“源域风格”图像（mask→image）。推理从高斯分布起点出发，降低对域特定纹理的依赖、强调结构一致性。流水线：1) 用未标注目标图像，通过SFM产生分割伪标签；2) 以伪标签经同一流合成源风格的合成图，形成“生成回放缓冲”；3) 进行生成式自训练：在真实目标图像与合成源样式图的联合数据上微调模型，从而锚定源域知识并提升目标域性能。

Result: 在腹部多器官与心脏分割（涵盖MRI↔CT跨模态）以及多站点前列腺分割任务上评测：新方法整体优于主流SFUDA基线，并与需要访问源数据的传统UDA方法性能接近。

Conclusion: 利用对称流匹配实现统一的分割-生成框架，在SFUDA场景下通过从高斯起点的结构化推理与生成回放自训练，稳定伪标签并保留源知识，显著提升跨模态与跨站点分割性能，缩小与常规UDA的性能差距。

Abstract: Domain shift across imaging modalities and acquisition sites remains a significant barrier to the clinical deployment of segmentation models. Source-free unsupervised domain adaptation (SFUDA) addresses this by adapting a pretrained model to an unlabeled target domain without requiring access to sensitive source data. We introduce a novel SFUDA framework built on Symmetrical Flow Matching, a unified generative model that segments an input image and synthesizes a source-like image from a mask within the same learned flow. By initializing inference from a domain-agnostic Gaussian origin, the model preserves structural consistency across domains and grounds predictions in learned anatomy rather than shifted texture statistics. Our pipeline leverages this symmetry to generate reliable pseudo-labels and corresponding source-like synthetic images from unlabeled target data, creating a generative replay buffer that anchors source knowledge during a generative self-training stage that fine-tunes on a joint set of real target and synthetic source-like images. We evaluate on abdominal multi-organ and cardiac segmentation, covering cross-modality MRI<->CT shifts, and multi-site prostate segmentation. Our approach outperforms SFUDA baselines and is competitive with conventional UDA methods.

</details>


### [102] [Vision-Language Models for Occupational Physical Exposure Assessment: Estimating External Hand Forces in Manual Material Handling Tasks from RGB Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22586)
*Mohammad Sadra Rajabi,Aanuoluwapo Ojelade,Sunwook Kim,Maury A. Nussbaum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出并评估了一条基于视觉-语言模型的流水线，仅用RGB视频与已知箱体质量即可连续估计双侧、三轴外部手部作用力，跨多视角与ROI策略取得约5–11 N的误差，显示在无需佩戴或物体传感器的条件下进行可扩展的人体工效与风险评估的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业人因与工伤风险评估需要连续、方向分解的外部手力曲线，但传统方法常依赖加装传感器或仪器化物体，成本高、部署难、可扩展性差。作者希望用仅基于视频与任务先验（如载荷质量）的AI方法，低成本、无接触地估计动态手部外力。

Method: 构建VLM管线：借助任务文本提示进行参与者与被搬运箱体的ROI定位；使用预训练ViT提取视觉特征；以变换器进行时间回归，输出双手三轴连续力；比较四种ROI策略（含/不含物体、像素级分割等）与七种单/多机位配置；在35名受试者、5类MMH任务（提举、搬运、推、拉）与三种箱重（6/9/12 kg）上，采用留一被试交叉验证评估。

Result: 整体RMS误差：水平与左右向约4.7–5.6 N，垂直向约10.6–11.0 N；将“被搬运物体”作为第二ROI通常提升性能，单机位条件下收益更明显；像素级分割增益有限；多机位对峰值力估计提升最明显，尤其是垂直分量；不同机位在逐帧误差上的差异较小。

Conclusion: 仅依赖RGB视频与已知载荷质量即可获得连续、双侧、定向的手部外力估计，无需在工人或物体上布置传感器，验证了面向更大规模职业体力暴露与风险评估的可行路径。

Abstract: External hand forces are important inputs to biomechanical analyses of occupational physical exposure and injury risk, yet continuous force measurements during manual material handling (MMH) typically requires instrumented objects or specialized sensing. We evaluated a vision-language model (VLM)-based pipeline that combines task-specific textual cues, visual representations, and known box mass to estimate dynamic, triaxial, bilateral external hand forces from RGB video. Thirty-five healthy young adults performed five MMH tasks involving lifting, carrying, pushing, and pulling with box masses of 6, 9, and 12 kg. The pipeline used text-guided localization of participant and handled-object regions of interest (ROIs), pretrained vision-transformer feature extraction, and transformer-based temporal regression. Performance was evaluated using leave-one-subject-out validation across seven camera-view conditions (three single-view and four multi-view conditions) and four ROI strategies. Overall, root mean square error was ~4.7-5.6 N for the horizontal and mediolateral force components and ~10.6-11.0 N for the vertical component. Including the handled object as a second ROI generally improved force estimation, with some of the largest benefits under single-camera conditions, whereas pixel-level segmentation provided little additional improvement. Multi-camera capture provided the clearest benefit for peak-force estimation, particularly for the vertical component, whereas differences in overall frame-level error among camera configurations were comparatively modest. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of estimating continuous, bilateral, directional hand-force estimates from RGB video and known load mass without requiring sensors on the worker or handled objects as model inputs, supporting the development of more scalable occupational physical exposure and risk assessments.

</details>


### [103] [AI-based worker guidance in assembly and disassembly operations using multimodal ego/exo-centric data capture and structured task knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22617)
*Vivek Chavan,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从专家示范的视频与旁白中自动抽取装/拆卸任务知识的资料导向方法，生成结构化流程表示，用于文档化与情境化指导；在真实拆解案例中优于仅基于静态图像的方法，显示以第一人称视频理解在维修、培训与循环制造上的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配/拆解知识依赖专家经验，难以系统记录、复用与传承；现有基于静态图像或手工规则的方法难以捕捉时序与语境信息，限制了程序化文档与现场指导能力。

Method: 采集第一人称（egocentric）与第三人称（exocentric）视频及口述旁白，联合编码时序与多模态信息，自动抽取步骤、动作、对象与上下文关系，形成结构化任务表示；以此驱动程序性文档生成与情境感知的工人指导。

Result: 在真实世界拆解案例中评测，基于视频的表示能捕捉过程结构与执行情境，性能与可用性均优于仅用静态图像的方法。

Conclusion: 多模态、时序感知的第一人称视频理解可有效外化与传递装/拆卸知识，支持维修、培训与循环制造等应用；该路线具备实际潜力并优于传统静态视觉方案。

Abstract: Assembly and disassembly processes rely on expert knowledge that is difficult to document, reuse, and transfer. This paper presents a data-centric approach for extracting structured task knowledge from expert demonstrations using egocentric and exocentric recordings. Temporal and multimodal information from video and narration is jointly encoded to derive structured task representations that enable procedural documentation and context-aware worker guidance. The approach is evaluated on a real-world disassembly case study, demonstrating that video-based representations capture procedural structure and execution context beyond static image-based methods. The results highlight the potential of egocentric video understanding for repair, training, and circular manufacturing applications. Project website: https://indego-assistant.github.io/

</details>


### [104] [OmniCAD: A Large-Scale Benchmark for 3D Spatial Reasoning in Robotics Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22637)
*Mingjia Wang,Taiting Lu,Ziwei Dong,Sisong Bei,Jingying Zeng,Runze Liu,Kaiyuan Lin,Hongxing Pan,Kai Zhang,Yizheng Hou,Yangshoudu Zheng,Chenchen Guo,Weiyuan Meng,Shubin Lyu,Zhijun Zheng,Dexu Wang,Xinyu Bai,Shurui Qian,Zhangzixin,Mengyu Pan,Guoliang Shi,Ling Ma,Yifan Yang,Qi He,Yi-Chao Chen,Yincheng Jin,Sung-Liang Chen,Mahanth Gowda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniCAD 是一个面向复杂机械装配的多模态/视觉语言模型评测基准，涵盖25k个工业级装配（平均12个零件、21类配合关系），提供真值3D模型与20视角渲染，用于系统性测评VLM在三类能力上的表现：零件位姿推理、零件间配合/约束识别、以及带工具的交互式视角选择与迭代推理。实验证明现有VLM在工业装配场景中易出现位姿不准、配合无效、零件互穿，且随复杂度上升性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM在常见视觉理解与空间推理上表现强劲，但其在需严格物理与几何一致性的机械装配理解方面缺乏系统检验与数据资源。工业级装配涉及精确位姿、约束类型与装配序关系，对模型提出更高的可解释性与物理有效性要求，因此需要一个标准化、可复现实验平台推动该方向发展。

Method: 构建大规模跨行业机械装配数据集：为每个装配提供人校验的3D真值、20视角渲染与标注的21类配合（mate）关系；设计三大评测任务：1) 组件级3D位姿估计（位置+姿态）；2) 部件间关系/约束识别（配合类型与装配约束）；3) 工具增强的代理式推理流程，让模型可选择视角、检查证据并迭代修正预测；提供评测代码与工具接口以标准化比较。

Result: 在OmniCAD上，现有VLM在工业装配推理上整体表现不佳：位姿误差偏大、配合关系判定错误频发、出现零件互穿，且随着零件数量与结构复杂度增加，性能明显退化。

Conclusion: OmniCAD揭示了当前VLM在机械装配理解上的显著短板，并通过开放数据、评测与工具为社区提供系统化基准，旨在促进更准确、物理一致、可扩展的3D装配推理方法研究。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) show strong capabilities in robotic perception and spatial reasoning, yet their ability to reason about complex mechanical assemblies remains underexplored. We introduce OmniCAD, a large-scale benchmark for assembly-aware 3D spatial reasoning across diverse industrial systems, including robotic mechanisms, automotive components, aerospace structures, and agricultural machinery.
