OmniCAD contains 25k mechanical assemblies, with an average of 12 parts per assembly and 21 types of mate relationships. Each assembly includes a human-verified ground-truth 3D model and renderings from 20 viewpoints. The benchmark evaluates three capabilities: (1) component-level 3D spatial reasoning, requiring prediction of part positions and orientations; (2) part-to-part relational reasoning, requiring identification of mating relationships and assembly constraints; and (3) tool-augmented agentic reasoning, where models iteratively select viewpoints, inspect visual evidence, and refine predictions. Experiments show that current VLMs struggle with industrial assembly reasoning, often producing inaccurate poses, invalid mating relationships, part interpenetration, and degraded performance as assembly complexity increases. We will open-source the benchmark, evaluation code, and tool interfaces to support research on accurate, physically valid, and scalable 3D assembly reasoning.

</details>


### [105] [Multiple View Neural Regression of a Facial Shape Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22655)
*Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出从多视角合成图像直接回归可动画的重拓扑人脸网格，以减少传统高质量面部捕捉的人工与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量面部动画需要重拓扑网格，但传统通过硬件阵列与繁琐处理获取，成本高、流程慢。作者希望用更高效、可扩展的方法，降低对专用设备与大量人工的依赖，同时保持生产级质量。

Method: 1) 构建VarIS光球，获取高分辨率双目几何与反射率；2) 系统性分析相机内外参对2D/3D自动landmark的影响；3) 用物理真实渲染器Visage Craft与A3DMM生成多视角合成数据，训练深度网络直接回归重拓扑面部网格；4) 在神经多视角回归中加入精确相机参数与3D landmark正则化以提升几何一致性与重建质量。

Result: 所提系统能以极少人工监督输出可直接绑定与动画的标准化人脸网格。实验显示：精确的相机内外参显著提升landmark精度与几何一致性；加入3D landmark正则化进一步提升重建质量。

Conclusion: 结合物理准确的合成数据与对相机参数、3D landmark约束的建模，可在无需昂贵捕捉流程的情况下高效产出生产级重拓扑人脸网格，为规模化面部动画制作提供可行路径。

Abstract: Creating re-topologized 3D facial meshes is essential for high-quality facial animation but remains labor-intensive and time-consuming. This dissertation explores more efficient approaches for capturing production-ready facial meshes through: (1) the development of VarIS, a custom light sphere for capturing high-resolution stereo geometry and reflectance maps; (2) analysis of camera parameters affecting automatic 2D and 3D landmarking; (3) synthetic-data methods for training neural face regression; and (4) techniques for improving neural multi-view face-shape regression.
  While VarIS enables photorealistic face capture, its operational and processing costs motivate a more scalable approach. A deep learning framework is therefore proposed to directly predict re-topologized facial meshes from synthetic multiview images generated with Visage Craft, an in-house physically based rendering system using an Appearance 3D Morphable Model (A3DMM). The system produces standardized meshes ready for rigging and animation with minimal human supervision. Results show that incorporating accurate camera intrinsics and extrinsics improves landmark accuracy and geometric consistency, while 3D landmark regularization further improves reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [106] [Hyperbolic Hierarchical Clustering for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22665)
*Jianan Wei,Guikun Chen,Zhiyuan Weng,Chunchao Guo,Yujia Wang,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ClusterMixer与HCFormer：用层次聚类（在双曲空间）作为可解释的Token Mixer，跨多视觉任务优于现有骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流Token Mixer（卷积/注意力/MLP/混合）在精度-计算权衡上有效，但编码过程不透明、可解释性差。作者希望用一种本质可解释的方式实现跨patch的信息交互。

Method: 将Token混合作为显式的层次聚类过程来建模：在双曲空间进行聚类以捕捉视觉数据的树状/层级关系，降低层级嵌入畸变；据此设计ClusterMixer，并构建集成该模块与若干聚类策略的新骨干HCFormer。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、实例分割与语义分割等多任务上，HCFormer取得一致优于对手的性能（实验广泛）。

Conclusion: 基于双曲空间层次聚类的ClusterMixer提供透明、可解释的Token混合机制；HCFormer在多任务上效果强，有望推动可解释视觉骨干的范式转变。

Abstract: We investigate the token mixer in vision backbones by revisiting clustering, one of the most classic approaches in machine learning. An effective token mixer is a fundamental component of modern vision backbones like vision Transformers, facilitating information exchange between image patches. Mainstream token mixers, which rely on convolution, attention, MLP, or their hybrids, primarily focus on navigating the trade-off between accuracy and computational cost. However, a significant drawback of these methods is their black-box nature; their encoding process is opaque and lacks interpretability. Diverging from these opaque designs, we introduce ClusterMixer, a transparent token mixer that is grounded in a clustering paradigm and interpretable by design. ClusterMixer explicitly formulates the token mixing process through a hierarchical clustering mechanism. To model the natural, tree-like relationships inherent in visual data, the clustering is performed in hyperbolic space, which is well-suited for embedding hierarchies with low distortion. Building on this innovation, we present HCFormer, a new backbone architecture that integrates ClusterMixer with a series of meticulously designed clustering strategies to ensure robust performance across tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HCFormer consistently outperforms its counterparts across diverse tasks, including image classification, object detection, instance segmentation, and semantic segmentation. Considering its transparency and efficacy, we hope HCFormer can facilitate a paradigm shift toward interpretable backbones.

</details>


### [107] [Contextrast++: Robust Multi-Scale Contextual Contrastive Learning for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22679)
*Changki Sung,Hyungtae Lim,Wanhee Kim,Youngwoo Seo,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Contextrast++，通过上下文对比学习与边界感知负样本采样，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升语义分割精度，兼顾多尺度上下文融合与长尾类别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义分割难以同时高效建模局部与全局上下文，且在长尾类别分布下表现受限；需要一种既能改进多尺度特征融合又能缓解类别不平衡的方法。

Method: 方法由两部分构成：1) 上下文对比学习（CCL），包含自适应融合模块、像素到锚点（PA）损失、锚点到锚点（AA）损失。自适应融合动态平衡局部与全局特征；PA基于融合后的多尺度特征进行像素-锚点对比以提升表征；AA利用一个类别均衡的记忆库进行锚点间对比以缓解长尾分布。2) 边界感知负样本（BANE）采样，从被误分类的边界区域选取困难负样本，强化细粒度边界学习。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，相比现有对比学习的SOTA方法显著提升分割性能；且推理阶段无额外计算开销。

Conclusion: Contextrast++通过自适应多尺度上下文融合、记忆库驱动的类均衡对比与边界困难负样本采样，有效提升语义分割的精度与对长尾分布的鲁棒性，并保持推理效率不变。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation has rapidly advanced with deep learning; however, challenges remain in effectively capturing local and global contexts as well as addressing the long-tailed distribution problem. To tackle these issues, we present Contextrast++, a robust contrastive learning method for semantic segmentation that improves multi-scale feature integration and mitigates class imbalance issues. Our method consists of two key components: 1) contextual contrastive learning (CCL) and 2) boundary-aware negative (BANE) sampling. CCL includes three subcomponents: adaptive fusion module, pixel-to-anchor (PA) loss, and anchor-to-anchor (AA) loss. The adaptive fusion module dynamically balances local and global feature integration, resulting in a more context-aware representation. While the PA loss leverages the fused multi-scale features to improve feature representation learning, the AA loss focuses on addressing the long-tailed distribution problem by utilizing a memory bank that stores a fixed number of class-balanced representative anchors. Meanwhile, BANE sampling enhances segmentation precision by selecting hard negatives from misclassified boundary regions, which refines fine-grained details during contrastive learning. As verified in extensive experiments using public datasets, we demonstrate that Contextrast++ substantially improves semantic segmentation performance over existing contrastive learning-based state-of-the-art approaches, while introducing no additional computational overhead during inference.

</details>


### [108] [MorphoCLIP: Text-Supervised Contrastive Learning for Perturbation Matching in Cell Painting Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22690)
*Sukhrobbek Ilyosbekov,Shubham Gajjar,Rongfei Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphoCLIP：冻结视觉与文本骨干，仅训练轻量跨通道与投影层，在单张消费级GPU上，将Cell Painting形态学表型与化合物/CRISPR/ORF文本描述进行对齐，双向检索Top-10显著优于随机；复制对齐损失提升重复一致性，但基因-化合物匹配仍未解决；基因感知标签与板效应校正未带来稳定收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cell Painting成像能捕获化学或遗传扰动的细胞形态变化，但因生物效应细微与技术噪声大，难以将图像与具体扰动相连接；若能用文本监督把图像与扰动描述对齐，可使大规模成像筛选更易检索与解释。

Method: 提出对比学习模型MorphoCLIP：保持视觉与语言骨干冻结，仅训练小型跨通道融合模块与投影层；在CPJUMP1数据上，以图搜文与以文搜图；加入复制（重复实验）对齐损失；尝试基因感知标签与板位校正；评估Top-10检索率相对随机基线。

Result: MorphoCLIP在双向检索上Top-10命中率显著高于随机；复制对齐损失提高了跨重复的一致性，但提升未转化为可靠的基因-化合物匹配；基因感知标签与板效应校正对检索没有稳定收益。

Conclusion: 文本监督有助于组织与检索化学与遗传的Cell Painting数据，但将化合物与基因扰动进行可靠匹配仍是未解难题；轻量冻结骨干的对比框架在资源受限条件下可行。

Abstract: Cell Painting microscopy captures how cells change after a chemical or genetic perturbation. Connecting these images to the perturbations that produced them could make large imaging screens easier to search and interpret, but the task remains difficult because biological effects are subtle and technical variation is substantial. We introduce MorphoCLIP, a contrastive model that links Cell Painting profiles with text descriptions of compounds, CRISPR knockouts, and ORF overexpressions. The model keeps its vision and language backbones frozen and trains only a compact cross-channel module and projection layers, so it can be trained on a single consumer GPU. On held-out CPJUMP1 data, MorphoCLIP searches in both directions: from a cell image to its perturbation description and from a description to matching cell images. In both cases, a correct match appears among the top ten results much more often than expected by chance. Adding a replicate-alignment loss makes profiles from repeated experiments more consistent, although this improvement does not yet translate into reliable gene-compound matching. Gene-aware labels and plate correction also show no consistent retrieval benefit. These findings suggest that text supervision can help organize chemical and genetic Cell Painting data. Matching compounds with genetic perturbations, however, remains an open problem.

</details>


### [109] [Hybrid Generative-Discriminative Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22692)
*Siyuan Zhou,Li Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于图像合成中对象放置（位置、尺度）预测的半生成式方法：在背景上均匀布置锚点，融合前景与背景特征，对每个锚点打合理性分，并对正样本锚点预测可行放置集合；在OPA数据集上兼顾效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式方法效果好但效率低，判别式方法效率高但效果受限，二者难以兼顾；需要一种既高效又有效的对象放置策略，用于前景对象在背景中的合理位置与尺度预测。

Method: 在背景图上均匀撒布锚点；提取并融合前景与背景特征；为每个锚点评分其放置合理性（打分筛选正锚点）；对被判为正的锚点进一步预测其可行的放置参数集合（位置与尺度）。

Result: 在OPA数据集上进行了大量实验，所提方法在效率与效果之间取得较好平衡，优于或可比于现有生成式与判别式方法的综合表现。

Conclusion: 半生成式的锚点打分与放置集合预测框架能有效提升对象放置任务的实用性，在保证较高效率的同时保持竞争性准确度。

Abstract: As an important operation of image composition, object placement aims to predict the plausible placement (location, scale) for the inserted foreground object. Previous object placement methods can be divided into generative methods and discriminative methods, both of which cannot balance efficiency and effectiveness well. In this work, we propose a semi-generative method in the middle ground between them. In particular, we assign uniformly distributed anchors on the background. Then, we fuse foreground and background features to predict the rationality score for each anchor and predict plausible placement sets for positive anchors. Extensive experiments on the OPA dataset show that our method can strike a good balance between efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [110] [LoViF 2026 The First Challenge on Unified Removal of Raindrops and Reflections: Methods and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22723)
*Zewei He,Xi Tong,Yu Chen,Xingyu Liu,Xin Li,Zepeng Wang,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Yuxuan Chen,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou,Minmin Yi,Chuanrui Zhang,Liwen Zhang,Yeongjin Jeong,Hyunjin Cho,Jiwon Lee,Minsang Kim,Jae Woong Soh,Jin-Hui Jiang,Rong-Lin Jian,Chih-Chung Hsu,Youngjin Oh,Junhyeong Kwon,Junyoung Park,Jae Hyun Park,Sung Ju Lee,Nam Ik Cho,Vishwajeet Shukla,Himanshu Baurai,Zhiqi Zhang,Kui Jiang,Zhaocheng Yu,Runzhe Li,Dawei Fan,Hao Li,Zhanshuo Zhang,Fan Ji,Jiangmeng Li,Xiongxin Tang,Fanjiang Xu,Shangquan Sun,Anh-Kiet Duong,Petra Gomez-Krämer,Jean-Michel Carozza,Ruibo Zhang,Dexiang Hong,Xinyan Liu,Shengeng Tang,Weidong Chen,Tzu-Hsuan Weng,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于“统一去除雨滴与反射”挑战赛的综述性工作坊论文：总结赛题、数据、参赛方法与结果，并提炼有效策略与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶常遇到雨天挡风玻璃/摄像头画面同时受雨滴与玻璃/路面反射干扰，导致感知退化。过去方法多分别处理雨滴或反射，缺乏统一解决方案与统一评测。为推动该方向，需要公开数据集、标准评测与方法对比。

Method: 组织并评测“统一去除雨滴与反射”挑战赛：提供真实拍摄的RDRF数据集（含RainDrop与ReFlection子集），收集149名注册、12份有效提交与技术说明；基于统一协议评测各方法表现，并对策略、网络设计、损失函数与训练细节进行对比分析。

Result: 挑战赛收获12个有效方案；基于RDRF上的统一指标完成系统评测，报告中归纳了哪些方法更有效，并呈现定量与定性结果与错误分析，产出对任务的经验洞见。

Conclusion: 统一去除雨滴与反射是实际需求强的难题；比赛显著推动了该方向的发展。真实数据与统一评测促进了方法进步与可复现性；报告总结了有效做法并指出未来研究的潜在路径。

Abstract: This workshop paper comprehensively reviews the First Challenge on Unified Removal of Raindrops and Reflections. The challenge aims to address a frequently encountered practical problem in the field of autonomous driving, i.e., raindrop-reflection composite degradation on rainy days. This competition attracted 149 registered participants and received 12 valid final submissions with corresponding fact sheets, significantly contributing to the progress of unified removal of raindrops and reflections. All the methods are developed and evaluated on our real-shot RainDrop and ReFlection (RDRF) dataset. A detailed analysis of the submitted methods and corresponding results is provided in this report, which highlights effective approaches and provides interesting insights for future research.

</details>


### [111] [Seeing the Unseen: Semantic-in-Gaussian for Sparse-View 3D Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22740)
*Zeyang Bai,Yunpeng Wang,Yunbiao Wang,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SeeU：一种在稀疏视角下提升新视角合成质量与结构完整性的通用3D高斯泼洒（G-3DGS）框架，通过语义引导的高斯残差更新，显著改善遮挡/部分可见区域；在外推设置下较最新SOTA平均PSNR提升2.44 dB，保持高效前向推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有G-3DGS多依赖像素对齐的高斯估计，面对部分观测与遮挡区域易出现表面不完整与结构坍塌；需要一种能在稀疏视角下利用更强先验（如语义）与跨视几何信息的机制，既补全欠约束结构又不破坏表面一致性，并维持高效推理。

Method: 核心理念为“Semantic-in-Gaussian”：在高斯空间进行语义条件化细化。1) 设计跨视熵感知（CEA）模块，将多视图的语义与几何线索聚合为紧凑嵌入，并用熵感知衡量不确定性/信息量；2) 条件高斯Transformer接受这些嵌入，对粗高斯执行残差式更新，重点修复部分可见与遮挡处的欠约束区域，同时保留整体表面一致性；3) 整体为通用G-3DGS框架，前向推理仍高效。

Result: 在多个基准上，渲染质量与结构完整性稳定提升，特别是外推场景下相较近期SOTA G-3DGS平均PSNR提升2.44 dB；同时保持推理阶段的高效性。

Conclusion: 通过在高斯表示中引入语义条件与跨视不确定性感知，SeeU能在稀疏与外推设置中恢复不可见/部分可见结构，提升图像质量与几何完整性且不牺牲效率，具备良好的通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (G-3DGS) has emerged as a promising approach for novel view synthesis undersparse-view settings. However, existing frameworks remain restricted by pixel-aligned Gaussian estimation, whichstruggles in partially observed or occluded regions and often leads to incomplete surfaces or structural collapse. Toaddress these challenges, we propose SeeU (Seeing the Unseen), a novel G-3DGS framework. We frame its core design asSemantic-in-Gaussian: semantic-conditioned refinement in Gaussian space. Specifically, we introduce a Cross-viewEntropy-Aware (CEA) module that aggregates multi-view semantic and geometric cues into compact embeddings. Theseembeddings guide the Conditional Gaussian Transformer, which applies residual updates to coarse Gaussians, helpingrecover under-constrained regions of partially observed structures while preserving surface consistency. Comprehensiveexperiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SeeU consistently improves rendering quality and structuralcompleteness while retaining efficient feed-forward inference. Especially under challenging extrapolation settings,SeeU achieves an average improvement of 2.44 dB in PSNR compared to recent SOTA G-3DGS methods.

</details>


### [112] [Object-Uni: A Unified Model for Object-Centric Spatial Understanding and Controllable Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22757)
*Mining Tan,Yinuo Wang,Ziqi Zhou,Weize Quan,Sifei Li,Jingdong Chen,DanDan Zheng,Libin Wang,Weiming Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Object-Uni统一模型，将物体姿态作为显式几何变量，连接姿态感知、空间推理、姿态条件生成与物体中心新视角合成，并配套UniSpatial-80K数据集与对象-Token-姿态锚训练。结果显著提升物体级姿态理解与可控生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一视觉理解与生成模型能描述物体但难以精确表示连续姿态，导致在目标视角下几何不一致、难以操控物体空间状态。需要一种能把姿态作为跨理解与生成共享的可计算几何表示的方法。

Method: 1) 将“物体中心的空间智能”统一为四项任务：姿态感知、空间推理、姿态条件生成、物体中心新视角合成；2) 把物体姿态设为显式几何变量，在理解与生成端共享；3) 提出基于视点的朝向抽象，把连续朝向映射为结构化视点描述，同时保留连续几何监督；4) 构建UniSpatial-80K基准；5) 通过对象token对齐的姿态锚，关联每个实例与其姿态；6) 训练一个统一多模态大模型。

Result: 实验表明该模型在物体级姿态理解、基于姿态的可控图像生成以及新视角合成方面优于现有统一模型，实现更高的几何一致性与可控性。

Conclusion: 将姿态作为统一的显式几何变量并引入视点式朝向抽象与对象-token姿态锚，可有效弥合理解与生成之间的空间表征鸿沟，推动统一模型从“会描述”走向“会操控”物体空间状态。

Abstract: Unified models for visual understanding and generation have made rapid progress, yet they still lack the ability to understand and manipulate the spatial states of object instances. Existing models can describe objects in natural language, but they struggle to precisely represent continuous object poses and generate geometrically consistent images under target viewpoints. To mitigate this, we propose \emph{Object-Uni}, a unified model for object-centric spatial understanding and controllable generation. Specifically, we formulate object-centric spatial intelligence as a unified problem connecting pose perception, spatial reasoning, pose-conditioned generation, and object-centric novel view synthesis. We treat object pose as an explicit geometric variable shared by understanding and generation, rather than merely a prediction label or control signal. To make pose usable by multimodal large language models, we propose a viewpoint-based orientation abstraction that maps orientation into structured viewpoint descriptions while preserving continuous geometric supervision. We further construct an object-centric spatial benchmark (UniSpatial-80K) and train a unified model with an object-token-grounded pose anchor to associate each instance with its pose state. Experiments show that our model improves object-level pose understanding and pose-controllable generation, moving unified models from describing objects toward manipulating spatial states.

</details>


### [113] [ByteAction: Byte-space Action Recognition Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22760)
*Fangcheng Li,Zhen Yu,Kejun Wu,Qiong Liu,You Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ByteAction，一种直接在压缩比特流上进行动作识别的基础模型，通过双视图字节级框架与一致性训练，在受损比特流上取得SOTA鲁棒性，同时保持完好比特流上的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动作识别依赖解码后的像素，存在隐私泄露、对文件完整性敏感、对比特流损伤脆弱的问题。需要一种无需像素重建、能在受损比特流上稳健工作的动作识别方法。

Method: 提出ByteAction：1) 双视图字节级框架，从同一比特流构造弱/强损伤视图；2) 共享的ByteFormer骨干在字节空间编码；3) Bitstream Pattern Augmentation（BPA）：将1D字节序列重排为2D字节矩阵并做区域级擦除，促使学习跨区域依赖；4) 损伤一致性训练（Corruption Consistency Training）：对不同损伤强度的预测施加双向KL约束；5) 联合分类与一致性目标优化。

Result: 在Stanford40、PPMI、PASCAL VOC 2012 Action的图像比特流上，ByteAction在所有场景的损伤鲁棒性达到SOTA，同时在完好比特流上保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 在无需像素解码的字节空间中，通过BPA与一致性训练，ByteAction实现对受损比特流的人体动作识别的强鲁棒与高精度，适用于隐私敏感与易受损环境。

Abstract: Byte-space Action Recognition (BAR) aims to recognize human actions directly from compressed image bitstreams without any pixel decoding. By operating entirely in byte space, BAR is inherently independent of file integrity and pixel-level reconstruction, making it naturally applicable to privacy-sensitive scenarios and robust against bitstream corruption. In this paper, we propose ByteAction, a BAR foundation model that achieves accurate action recognition on corrupted image bitstreams. ByteAction follows a dual-view byte-level recognition framework. It constructs weakly and strongly corrupted bitstream views, which are augmented by Bitstream Pattern Augmentation (BPA) and encoded with a shared ByteFormer backbone. The model is optimized with both classification and corruption consistency objectives. Specifically, we propose Bitstream Pattern Augmentation (BPA), which reshapes one-dimensional byte sequences into two-dimensional byte matrix and applies region-level erasure to encourage the model to learn robust cross-region byte dependencies. We further propose a Corruption Consistency Training strategy that constrains the model to maintain stable predictions across different corruption severities through bidirectional KL divergence. Experiments on the image bitstream from Stanford40, PPMI, and PASCAL VOC 2012 Action demonstrate that ByteAction achieves state-of-the-art corruption robustness across all scenarios while maintaining competitive intact bitstream performance.

</details>


### [114] [LagrangeGS: Non-Conservative Lagrangian System on Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22773)
*Shogo Sato,Takuhiro Kaneko,Shoichiro Takeda,Tomoyasu Shimada,Riku Inoue,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Ryuichi Tanida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LagrangeGS 将动态3D高斯泼溅建模为非保守拉格朗日系统，通过物理一致的动力学、时间无关的非保守力与局部刚性对齐，解决现有方法的轨迹不一致、不可逆与长期外推崩塌问题，实现稳定外推、可逆时间与物理编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3DGS尽管能重建照片级时变场景，但物理扩展仅拟合速度场，不满足拉格朗日力学，导致(1) 轨迹物理不一致，(2) 缺乏时间可逆性，(3) 长期外推几何崩塌；需要一个物理一致且可计算的动力学框架。

Method: 将动态3DGS表述为非保守拉格朗日系统：采用拉格朗日神经网络思想，但为避免对数百万粒子进行巨大速度Hessian求逆，近似速度Hessian为单位阵以解耦粒子动力学；将非保守力限制为显式与时间无关以实现可逆积分；引入局部刚性配准正则化粒子轨迹。

Result: 在多个动态场景基准上，LagrangeGS实现稳定的长期外推、一致的时间反演，并可在无需再训练的情况下进行基于物理的反事实编辑。

Conclusion: 物理一致的拉格朗日建模与计算可行的近似，使动态3DGS获得可逆、稳健、可编辑的长期时空行为，克服以往仅拟合速度场方法的三大缺陷。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves photorealistic reconstruction of time-varying scenes, and recent physics-aware extensions improve extrapolation by explicitly predicting velocity fields. However, these extensions merely fit vector fields to visual deformations without satisfying Lagrangian mechanics, leading to three major issues: (i) physically inconsistent trajectories, (ii) lack of time-reversibility, and (iii) geometric collapse during long-term extrapolation. In this paper, we propose LagrangeGS, which formulates dynamic 3DGS as a non-conservative Lagrangian system. While this Lagrangian formulation fundamentally solves (i), a direct application of general LNNs to dynamic 3DGS requires a large velocity-Hessian inversion for millions of Gaussian particles. To overcome this computational bottleneck, we approximate the velocity-Hessian as an identity matrix, decoupling particle dynamics for computational tractability. For (ii), we restrict the non-conservative forces to be explicitly time independent, enabling consistent backward integration. Finally, to address (iii), we introduce local rigid alignment that regularizes particle trajectories. Extensive evaluations on dynamic scene benchmarks demonstrate that LagrangeGS enables stable long-term extrapolation, consistent time reversal, and counterfactual physics-based editing without retraining.

</details>


### [115] [Can We Perform Online RL for Image Editing without Editing Rewards?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22780)
*Qichao Ma,Jikang Cheng,Ling Liang,Zhaofei Yu,Tiejun Huang,Renye Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在无专门编辑奖励的情况下，用T2I奖励体系来做图像编辑的RL：先学一个与奖励对齐的“反事实目标描述”生成器（captioner），再用冻结的captioner将编辑任务转化为T2I目标，最后仅凭T2I奖励优化编辑策略，效果与使用编辑奖励相当并优于直接迁移基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑的RL需要编辑特定的奖励，但收集/标定昂贵（如三元组监督、任务依赖调参）。相比之下，T2I已拥有成熟多样的奖励（语义对齐、美学、真实感、字形等）。若能将编辑问题映射到T2I奖励空间，可大幅降低成本并扩展可优化的视觉偏好范围。

Method: 提出Lever-Edit两阶段框架：1) 学习一个与T2I奖励对齐的captioner，生成可作为优化目标的“反事实”完整描述（将相对编辑指令转成自洽目标文本，同时对齐冻结的T2I奖励）；2) 冻结captioner，仅用T2I奖励（质量、对齐、审美等）对图像编辑策略进行RL优化。核心映射：质量直接迁移；遵循提示通过目标描述对齐；参考一致性通过对源内容的语义编码保留。

Result: 在多个实验中，方法在编辑对齐与源内容保留方面与基于编辑奖励的微调相当，同时明显优于直觉式的简单奖励迁移基线。

Conclusion: 图像编辑RL无需专门的编辑奖励也可行：通过学习与T2I奖励对齐的目标描述器并用T2I奖励优化，可获得具竞争力的编辑效果，降低监督与标定成本并扩展可利用的偏好空间。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables direct preference optimization for image editing through editing-specific rewards, which remain less developed due to costly triplet supervision and complex task-dependent calibration. In contrast, text-to-image (T2I) generation benefits from a mature and diverse reward ecosystem spanning semantic alignment, aesthetics, realism, glyph shape, and other visual preferences. Extending this ecosystem to image editing would substantially broaden the range of visual preferences accessible to RL-based optimization, prompting the central question: \emph{Can We Perform Image Editing RL without Editing Rewards?} In this paper, we argue that the standard image editing dimensions have potential to be mapped to the T2I reward space: image quality can transfer directly, prompt following can be aligned through a description of the desired visual state, and reference consistency admits a coarse semantic conversion by encoding the source content to preserve. However, editing instructions specify relative changes, whereas T2I rewards require self-contained target descriptions; moreover, semantically valid captions from generic vision-language models may be incompatible with the frozen reward. Hence, we further introduce Lever-Edit, a two-stage framework that learns a reward-aligned captioner for counterfactual target descriptions, freezes it, and optimizes the editing policy solely with the transferred T2I reward. Experiments show competitive editing alignment and source preservation against editing-reward-based fine-tuning, while outperforming intuitive transfer baselines.

</details>


### [116] [OmicSync: Reliability-Aware Spatial Multi-Omics Clustering with Evidence-Constrained LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22785)
*Rabeya Tus Sadia,Qiang Ye,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmicSync 是一个面向空间多组学的“可置信度解释”聚类与推理框架，利用模型产生的每点置信度/不确定性与模态权重，联合LLM生成可审计解释，并通过OmicSync-R以推理质量强化学习闭环进一步提升聚类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间域发现方法多只给出聚类标签，缺乏对每个点的可靠性评估、各模态贡献度、以及可审计的决策理由，导致结果难以信任与复现。

Method: 提出OmicSync：以KAN-GCN为骨干，结合空间编码、跨模态融合、不确定性感知路由、细胞类型监督与缺失模态补全；将每点的置信度、认识论不确定性、模态路由权重等转成结构化证据字典，驱动LLM进行标准/分步/反事实/对比/不确定性解释。提出OmicSync-R：以自动化推理质量分数作为REINFORCE奖励，不反传语言模型，闭环让“解释一致性”影响潜在结构。

Result: 在四个10x CytAssist FFPE空间蛋白组学基准上，OmicSync在Tonsil、Glioblastoma、Tonsil Add-on取得最佳平均名次（1.44/1.78/1.22），在Human Breast Cancer为第二（2.33）。OmicSync-R将该数据集ARI从45.73提升到46.72，并在九项聚类指标中六项领先。

Conclusion: OmicSync/OmicSync-R实现了面向不确定性的、可审计的点级空间域发现：既提升聚类性能，又提供由证据约束的可解释推理，并以推理质量反哺表征学习。

Abstract: Spatial multi-omics technologies jointly profile gene expression, surface proteins, and histology at each tissue spot, yet most spatial domain discovery methods provide only cluster assignments, without indicating assignment reliability, modality contributions, or why a domain decision should be trusted. We present OmicSync, a reliability-aware spatial multi-omics framework that couples unsupervised domain clustering with evidence-constrained LLM reasoning using model-derived per-spot signals, including assignment confidence, epistemic routing uncertainty, and modality-routing weights. These signals are converted into structured evidence dictionaries and used to generate standard, stepwise, counterfactual, contrastive, and uncertainty-focused explanations. OmicSync integrates a KAN-GCN backbone with spatial encoding, cross-modal fusion, uncertainty-aware routing, cell-type supervision, and missing-modality imputation. We further introduce OmicSync-R, which closes the reasoning-clustering loop by using automatically computed reasoning-quality scores as REINFORCE rewards, allowing reasoning coherence to shape the latent structure without backpropagating through the language model. Across four 10x CytAssist FFPE spatial proteomics benchmarks, OmicSync achieves the best average rank on Human Tonsil (1.44), Glioblastoma (1.78), and Tonsil Add-on (1.22), and second-best on Human Breast Cancer (2.33). OmicSync-R further improves ARI on Human Breast Cancer from 45.73 to 46.72 and outperforms existing methods on six of nine clustering metrics. Together, OmicSync and OmicSync-R enable reliability-aware, spot-level auditable spatial domain discovery guided by evidence-constrained reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [GuidedFlow: An Attention-Guided Framework for Anomaly Detection in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22789)
*Sosmita Paul,Krishna Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GuidedFlow：一种注意力引导的正态化流用于AM（3D打印）图像/视频异常检测，在小样本下更好检测微小/拉丝缺陷，跨时空建模，多数据集上优于现有方法（更高准确率与AUROC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AM质量管控难，打印缺陷与潜在网络物理入侵导致异常；现有重构/嵌入/流方法难以在小数据下泛化且对3D打印常见的微小/拉丝缺陷不敏感，需要能处理未见缺陷并具备时空建模与注意力聚焦的方案。

Method: 使用经领域微调的预训练ResNet提取特征；构建注意力引导的空间与时间正态化流框架，跨多尺度、多帧建模动力学；引入时空注意力网络（SAN）为流模型提供注意力，引导其聚焦于关键上下文区域，实现异常检测与定位。

Result: 在作者自建AM3D-AD（含良品与异常的3D打印图像/视频）和MVTec-AD数据集上，较多数SOTA方法取得更高的检测精度与AUROC，特别在微小/拉丝缺陷与小样本场景下表现更佳。

Conclusion: 注意力引导的正态化流（GuidedFlow）能有效提升AM场景的异常检测与定位能力，兼顾对未见缺陷的泛化与检测性能，并在标准与自建数据集上验证了优越性。

Abstract: Additive Manufacturing (AM) plays a vital role in the ongoing industrial revolution. However, quality control remains crucial and challenging due to printing defects or potential cyber-physical intrusions. Image or video-based anomaly detection is a key effort towards addressing these challenges. Various approaches have been explored in this domain, including reconstruction-based, embedding-based, and flow-based methods. Though normalizing flow-based methods address some of the core challenges of unforeseen defects and generalization while maintaining detection performance, existing approaches struggle with tiny/stringing defects common in 3D printing. In a small-data setting, this poses a limitation in generalization. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{GuidedFlow}, a novel attention-guided normalizing flow model for anomaly detection and localization. GuidedFlow employs a pre-trained ResNet model, fine-tuned on the domain dataset. An attention-guided spatial and temporal flow framework models the dynamics across multiple scales and frames. A Spatio-Temporal Attention Network (SAN) enables the flow model to prioritize relevant contextual cues from input frames. We evaluate GuidedFlow on our AM3D-AD dataset, consisting of benign and anomalous real 3D printed object images and videos. We also conduct a comparative study using the MVTec-AD industrial image anomaly detection dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that GuidedFlow outperforms most of the state-of-the-art models with enhanced detection accuracy and AUROC.

</details>


### [118] [VersaDB: A High-Performance AI Storage Database for Unifying Mutimodal Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22795)
*Cong Wang,Zelin Liu,Yang Luo Ran Zhang,Zhijian Guo,Hui Zhang,Fan Yu,Yanfei Cao,Naijie Gu,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VersaDB 是面向多模态 AI 数据集的统一数据库，采用分页存储与结构化/非结构化分离、B+ 树索引、自动分片与分层元数据管理，提供便捷转换 API，在多并行度下实现最高 5.35× 加速与稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态、多格式（文本、图像、音频、CSV、TFRecord、bin 等）数据在不同框架与硬件上因数据布局与处理方式不佳导致 I/O 瓶颈与吞吐不稳，需要统一存储与访问优化来匹配 GPU/TPU/NPU 的高训练吞吐。

Method: 设计 VersaDB：1) 分页式存储，结构化与非结构化数据分离；2) 基于 B+ 树的索引文件加速访问；3) 自动分片；4) 分层元数据（页/分片/全局）支撑高效运行；5) 提供将原始数据集和主流格式（CSV、TFRecord、.bin）转换为 VersaDB 的 API。

Result: 在实验中，VersaDB 相比现有处理方式最高可达 5.35× 的数据处理加速，并在不同并行度下保持一致且稳定的性能。

Conclusion: 统一的、多模态友好型数据库与分页+索引+分片+分层元数据的组合能够显著提升 AI 训练数据管线的访问效率与可扩展性，并减少因格式异构带来的吞吐波动。

Abstract: The AI field has been rapidly developing, leading to the emergence of a large number of AI training datasets of various types. These datasets contain different modalities, including text, images, audio, etc., and may come in various data storage formats. With the advancement of AI hardware, AI computation units like GPUs, TPUs, and NPUs can greatly accelerate the training speed of AI models, which in turn increases the demand for faster data processing. When using existing AI processing frameworks to handle datasets with different modalities and storage formats, processing speeds may be suboptimal due to issues such as data layout and the way users handle the data. Therefore, using a unified database to store multiple data formats can better manage and optimize data access. In this paper, we introduce VersaDB, a database designed specifically for AI datasets with various modalities. We implemented a page-based storage system, separating structured and unstructured data. Additionally, we generated B+ tree-based index files to accelerate data access. VersaDB supports automatic sharding and maintains a hierarchical metadata management system, with corresponding metadata maintained at the page, shard, and global levels, forming the foundation for the efficient operation of the database. We also focused on ease of use by providing APIs for directly converting datasets into VersaDB, as well as APIs for converting popular AI data storage formats (e.g., CSV, TFRecord, .bin) into VersaDB.Our experiments show that using VersaDB can achieve up to 5.35x acceleration and maintain consistent performance across different parallelism levels.

</details>


### [119] [Direct, Parallel, or Sequential? A Comparative Study of Training-Free Multi-Subject Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22819)
*Yanliang Qi,Kexi Chen,Muchao Ye,Haomiao Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了三种“免训练”的多主体图生视频(I2V)生成范式：直接、并行与顺序。它们在外观保真、动作分配、时空一致性与主体间交互上的权衡各异，并总结了各自的失败模式与实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有I2V在单主体上进步显著，但当场景包含多个主体时，需同时保持每个主体外观、分配不同动作并维持主体间时空互动，难度骤增。缺乏对不同无训练管线在多主体情形下系统性的对比与经验总结。

Method: 提出并统一定义三类免训练范式：1) 直接生成：用预训练I2V对整张参考图与完整文本一次性生成；2) 并行生成：将图与文本按主体分解，各主体独立生成后再时空合成；3) 顺序生成：先生成背景/场景，再按次序逐一引入主体，利用已生成的视频作为条件。基于多样多主体场景，评估外观保持、动作保真、时间一致性与主体间连贯性，并分析失败模式与敏感因素（如主体顺序与误差传播）。

Result: 实验表明：直接范式具备最强的全局上下文与主体互动，但在多主体复杂动作时易出现外观漂移与动作混淆；并行范式降低每步复杂度、主体外观更稳定，但主体间互动/遮挡与时序协调偏弱；顺序范式在场景连贯与遮挡关系上较好，但对主体引入顺序敏感，且前期错误会传播放大。

Conclusion: 三种范式各有取舍：需根据任务重心选择——若重互动与整体语义用直接；重外观控制与独立动作用并行并配合更强的合成/对齐；重场景一致与遮挡逻辑用顺序并谨慎设计顺序与误差抑制。论文给出实践建议以构建可控的多主体视频生成系统。

Abstract: Text-conditioned image-to-video (I2V) generation has advanced rapidly, yet generating videos with multiple subjects remains challenging. A model must simultaneously preserve the appearance of each subject, assign distinct motions, and maintain coherent spatial and temporal interactions. This paper presents a systematic study of three representative paradigms for training-free multi-subject I2V generation: direct, parallel, and sequential generation. Direct generation applies a pretrained I2V model to the complete reference image and prompt, requiring all subjects and motions to be synthesized jointly. Parallel and sequential generation instead decompose the reference image and prompt into subject-specific visual and textual conditions. Parallel generation synthesizes each subject independently and subsequently composes the resulting videos, reducing the complexity of each generation step at the cost of weaker inter-subject context. Sequential generation first synthesizes a background video and then progressively introduces individual subjects. This preserves accumulated scene context but introduces sensitivity to subject ordering and error propagation. We empirically evaluate the three paradigms across diverse multi-subject scenes, comparing appearance preservation, motion fidelity, temporal consistency, and inter-subject coherence, while also characterizing their distinct failure modes. Our findings reveal the strengths and limitations of each paradigm and offer practical insights for designing controllable multi-subject video generation systems.

</details>


### [120] [SiZeUp: Fast 3D Proxy from Aerial Images via Depth Ordinal Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22821)
*Wenjun Zhou,Yunshan Li,Qiaoyu Zhu,Weidan Xiong,Hao Zhang,Daniel Cohen-Or,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SiZeUp提出以“从足迹得高度”的低维优化框架，用可微渲染与序关系深度一致性监督，直接从校准的斜视航拍影像快速估计建筑高度并生成大规模城市代理模型，速度较SOTA提升23–52倍且保持相近覆盖与体积一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模城市3D代理重建多依赖特征匹配、密集点云或可靠的度量深度，计算昂贵且易受单目尺度歧义影响；需要一种既高效又稳健、能跨多视角一致的高度估计方法以支撑大规模建模。

Method: 将建筑表示为“从平面足迹挤出单一高度”的参数化代理，把3D抽象降为低维优化；用可微渲染把多视图中的代理映射为深度图；引入“序关系深度一致性损失”，仅约束相对深度次序以规避单目尺度歧义；并配合动态视角选择提高效率与稳健性，通过梯度将深度监督回传到建筑高度参数。

Result: 无需显式特征匹配或重建密集点云，即可获得与现有方法相当的代理级覆盖率与体积一致性；在多数据集上实现23–52×的速度提升。

Conclusion: 基于序关系深度一致性与可微渲染的低维挤出模型，可在大规模场景中高效、稳健地从斜视影像估计建筑高度，兼顾精度与速度，适合大规模城市建模任务。

Abstract: We present SiZeUp, a fast and scalable approach for constructing large-scale 3D urban proxy models directly from calibrated oblique aerial imagery. Our method adopts a height-from-footprint representation, reducing 3D building abstraction to a low-dimensional optimization problem in which building footprints are extruded by a single height parameter. To enable efficient and robust height estimation, we introduce an ordinal depth consistency loss that enforces agreement between the relative depth ordering of rendered proxies and depth priors predicted by a monocular depth model. This is realized through a differentiable renderer that maps parametric building proxies into multi-view depth images, allowing gradients to be propagated from depth supervision to building heights. Our ordinal formulation produces stable optimization in practice and avoids explicit feature matching or dense point cloud reconstruction. Rather than relying on metric depth, which can be unreliable under monocular scale ambiguity, our ordinal depth consistency loss operates on relative depths, providing a more reliable signal across views. Combined with an efficient dynamic view selection, our approach achieves a 23-52$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art proxy reconstruction pipelines while maintaining comparable proxy-level coverage and volume consistency, making it well suited for large-scale urban modeling tasks.

</details>


### [121] [VeCAS: Vessel-Focused Contrast-Free Angiogram Synthesis for Vascular Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22828)
*De-Xing Huang,Chen-Yu Wang,Hao Liang,Xiao-Hu Zhou,Mei-Jiang Gui,Tian-Yu Xiang,Qin-Yi Zhang,Chen Wang,Xiao-Liang Xie,Shi-Qi Liu,Ming-Yuan Liu,Zhen-Chang Wang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VeCAS：从无对比剂X光生成类血管造影图像的两阶段框架，先定位血管再在局部重建造影外观，显著提升结构保真与图像质量，并在机器人导航中提效约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统X线造影依赖碘造影剂，存在不良反应与使用限制；直接从无对比图像合成“造影样”图像有望替代，但现有方法血管定位可控性差、对冗余背景建模低效，影响结构保真与视觉真实感与下游应用。

Method: 两阶段框架VeCAS：Stage I以判别式模型在无对比X光中定位血管，并通过跨模态潜表示蒸馏从真实造影图像迁移“对血管敏感”的表征；Stage II在已定位的血管区域内使用“血管聚焦”的图像修补/填充模型合成造影外观，同时保持非血管背景不变。

Result: 在自建下肢血管介入数据集上，相比对比方法，结构保真与图像质量更优；主观视觉图灵测试与医师评估表明合成图像具备较高逼真度；在血管仿体中的机器人导丝导航实验，以VeCAS生成图像做引导，达成到达目标时间降低41.4%、操作步数降低40.7%。

Conclusion: VeCAS通过将血管定位与造影外观合成解耦，实现高保真、可控的无对比“类造影”生成，可作为“元对比剂”辅助血管介入与机器人导航，具有临床转化潜力。

Abstract: X-ray angiography relies on iodinated contrast agents to visualize vascular structures during image-guided interventions. However, contrast administration carries risks of adverse events, motivating the development of contrast-free alternatives. Generating X-ray angiograms directly from non-contrast X-ray images offers a potential solution, but existing approaches remain limited by (i) insufficient control over vascular localization and (ii) inefficient modeling of redundant background content. To address these challenges, we propose VeCAS, a two-stage vessel-focused contrast-free angiogram synthesis framework that separates vascular structure localization from angiographic appearance synthesis. In Stage I, a discriminative model localizes vascular structures in non-contrast X-ray images, while cross-modality latent distillation transfers vessel-sensitive knowledge from X-ray angiograms during training. In Stage II, a vessel-focused inpainting model synthesizes angiographic appearance within the localized vascular regions while preserving the non-vascular background. Experiments on an in-house lower-limb vascular intervention dataset show that VeCAS outperforms the comparison methods in terms of vascular structural fidelity and image quality. Visual Turing tests and physician assessments indicate the perceptual realism of the synthesized angiograms. In addition, robotic guidewire navigation experiments in vascular phantoms show that VeCAS guidance reduces the time to target by 41.4% and the number of operation steps by 40.7% compared with non-contrast guidance. Together, these results suggest the potential of VeCAS to serve as ``meta contrast agent'' for vascular interventions.

</details>


### [122] [Following Motion for Sequential Modeling in Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22861)
*Jaehyun Park,Nam Ik Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MGMVFI：用光流引导的Mamba式选择性状态空间模型做视频插帧，通过运动自适应序列化与上下文合成，在复杂大运动上达SOTA，且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM在VFI中可线性捕捉长程依赖，但固定扫描顺序难以对齐视频中随时间变化的运动轨迹，导致大/动态运动传播不一致；同时光流不准会带来特征不可靠。

Method: 1) 运动引导序列化(MGS)：用光流将2D特征重排为沿运动轨迹的一维输入序，令SSM的因果状态更新对齐语义相关token，实现运动一致的特征传播；2) 上下文合成：在光流不准时，利用周围空间上下文做稳健的跨帧特征合成；3) 定制Mamba骨干并加入轻量细化模块，低成本提升局部细节重建。

Result: 在标准VFI基准上取得SOTA，尤其在包含复杂和大幅运动的场景中表现显著更优，同时计算成本较低。

Conclusion: 将运动自适应序列化与上下文合成无缝融入Mamba式SSM，可有效对齐动态运动并稳健插帧，指向以序列建模视角解决视频插帧的新方向。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) have surfaced as a promising architecture in Video Frame Interpolation (VFI), as they can capture long-range dependencies with linear computational complexity. However, their predefined scanning order limits their effectiveness in modeling the dynamic motion trajectories inherent in VFI problems. To tackle this challenge, we propose Motion-Guided Mamba for Video Frame Interpolation (MGMVFI), an adaptation of the selective state space model tailored explicitly for VFI. MGMVFI introduces Motion-Guided Serialization (MGS), which leverages optical flow to define a motion-adaptive 1D input order for the SSM. This aligns the causal state updates with semantically related tokens, enabling motion-consistent feature propagation, particularly for large and dynamic motions. Additionally, to mitigate the unreliable feature representations caused by inaccurate optical flow estimates, we introduce contextual synthesis that utilizes the surrounding spatial context for robust inter-frame feature synthesis. These components are seamlessly integrated within our tailored Mamba architecture, which also employs a lightweight refinement block to enhance local detail reconstruction at a reduced computational cost. Extensive experiments on standard VFI benchmarks demonstrate that MGMVFI achievesstate-of-the-artperformance,particularly on complex and dynamic motions, thereby establishing a new direction for sequence modeling in video interpolation.

</details>


### [123] [Toward Sub-1 kB Identity-Preserving Face Compression: A Benchmark of Codecs, a Custom Learned Codec, and Studies of Resolution, Demographic Fairness, Recompression, and Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22866)
*Petr Hurtik,Jakub Sochor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在硬性≤1 KB的人脸图像存储预算下，通用编解码器难以保住“身份”这一验证关键特征。作者系统基准十种编解码器与多种人脸匹配器/分辨率/数据集，并提出可精确命中字节预算的身份保持型学习编解码器。结果显示：在1 KB@112px几乎可达近乎无损的身份匹配；但在512 B时排名重洗，传统与部分现代通用编解码器崩溃，而WebP、JPEG-AI与作者方法显著更稳。运营结论：1 KB下选的编解码器不适用于512 B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用（证件、智能卡、生物识别、低带宽核验）需将人脸图像压到亚千字节级，却要最大化身份可匹配性而非像素保真。现有通用编解码器优化MSE/感知质量，不直接优化人脸嵌入距离，且在极低码率下哪种编解码器/分辨率最能保住身份未知。

Method: - 基准：10种通用与人脸专用编解码器；多个分辨率与字节预算（≤1024 B, 512 B）；两数据集（Color FERET受控、AI-Solutions-KK野外）；四个锚定人脸匹配器；另以14个ViT/CNN模型验证排名与骨干无关。
- 训练自定义学习型“身份保持编解码器”：通过对冻结增益表做二分搜索精确命中字节预算。
- 四项研究：分辨率影响、公平性（人口统计学）、再压缩影响、无框对抗鲁棒性。

Result: - 1 KB@112px：问题基本“解决”，如在Color FERET上ArcFace的EER<0.35%。
- 512 B：排名重排。AVIF/HEIF/JPEG XL/JPEG在FMR=1e-4时的FNMR升至28%~98%（大幅劣化）；WebP、JPEG-AI与作者学习法保持较好，在野外集上WebP约24.3% FNMR，而作者高精度变体仅6.9%。
- 排名对主干网络基本不敏感。

Conclusion: 在亚千字节下“身份保留”可行，但强依赖预算：1 KB时多编解码器足够好，512 B时必须改选更稳健的方案（如WebP、JPEG-AI或作者的预算对齐学习编解码器）。运营指引：不要用1 KB下的最佳编解码器去部署512 B场景；需按目标预算与分辨率做专项选型，并考虑公平性、再压缩与对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Storing face images under a hard sub-kilobyte budget, as required for identity documents, smart-card biometrics and bandwidth-constrained verification, forces a codec to discard most of the signal while keeping what a face matcher actually reads: identity. Generic codecs optimize pixel fidelity, not the embedding distances that drive verification, so which codec, resolution and setting best preserve identity at 1024 bytes or less, and how that degrades at 512, is unclear.
  We benchmark ten general and face-specific codecs across resolutions, byte budgets, two datasets (controlled Color FERET, in-the-wild AI-Solutions-KK) and four anchor face matchers, with a fourteen-model ViT and CNN roster confirming the ranking is backbone-invariant. We then train a custom identity-preserving codec that hits the byte budget exactly via binary search over a frozen gain table, and run four studies: resolution, demographic fairness, recompression, and no-box adversarial robustness.
  Sub-kilobyte identity preservation is feasible, but which codec to deploy depends entirely on the budget. At 1024 bytes and the 112 px working resolution the problem is close to solved: modern codecs hold Color FERET equal-error rate under 0.35 percent on the ArcFace anchor. At 512 bytes the field re-sorts: AVIF, HEIF, JPEG XL and legacy JPEG collapse to 28 to 98 percent false-non-match rate at FMR 1e-4, while WebP, JPEG-AI and our byte-budgeted learned codecs stay out of that band, with 24.3 percent for WebP against 6.9 percent for our accurate variant in the wild. That re-sort, not the 1024-byte ranking, is the operational result: a codec chosen at 1 kB is not the codec to deploy at half that.

</details>


### [124] [Large-Small Model Collaboration for Zero-Shot Surgical Phase Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22879)
*Yiyi Zhang,Ying Zheng,Wenxin Fan,Yu Zhu,Yuchen Yuan,Litao Zhao,Zheng Li,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LaST框架：用大模型提供帧级先验，小模型进行时序精炼，通过质量控制与双模型交叉学习迭代优化，并循环回放强化监督，实现零样本外科手术阶段识别的稳健域适应，显著超越基线与少样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量时序模型擅长建模时间关系但在域迁移下泛化差；外科基础模型预训练强、可迁移但缺乏显式时序建模导致预测时间不一致。需要一种方法结合两者优势，实现对未见临床域的零样本阶段识别并保证时序一致与准确性。

Method: 提出Large-Small Temporal adaptation（LaST）：(1) 由大模型（FM）先生成帧级阶段先验作为弱监督；(2) 迭代式时序精炼，包含动态质量控制筛选可靠预测与双模型交叉学习以缓解确认偏差；(3) 轻量模型利用其时序能力逐步纠正不一致预测并提升精度；(4) 循环回放策略，将精炼后的更准标签作为下一轮监督信号，形成自增强闭环。

Result: 在零样本外科阶段识别的未见域上显著优于基线PeskaVLP，准确率提升24.85%–43.17%，并超过全监督线性探针和多种SOTA少样本方法。

Conclusion: 通过大-小模型协作与迭代自训练，LaST在保持迁移性的同时引入时序一致性，能够在无标注的新临床域实现稳健高精度的阶段识别，优于现有基线与少样本方案。

Abstract: Task-specific lightweight models for surgical phase recognition excel at capturing temporal dynamics but generalize poorly under domain shift. Conversely, surgical foundation models (FMs) offer superior transferability via large-scale pretraining, yet their lack of explicit temporal modeling often yields temporally inconsistent predictions, leading to degraded performance. To exploit the complementary strengths of both paradigms, we propose \textbf{La}rge-\textbf{S}mall \textbf{T}emporal adaptation (\textbf{LaST}), a novel large-small collaborative framework that enables zero-shot adaptation to unseen clinical domains. In LaST, the FM initiates the pipeline by generating frame-level phase priors that serve as initial weak supervision. To effectively utilize these noisy phase priors, we introduce an iterative temporal refinement scheme that integrates dynamic quality control to filter reliable predictions and dual-model cross-learning to mitigate confirmation bias. Simultaneously, the lightweight model leverages its intrinsic temporal modeling ability to progressively correct inconsistent predictions and enhance overall accuracy across iterations. At the end, a cycle replay strategy is employed to close the loop: the refined, more accurate predictions are utilized as upgraded supervision signals for the subsequent iterations, fostering a self-reinforcing evolution of both label quality and model capability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LaST achieves robust adaptation to unseen domains for zero-shot surgical phase recognition, outperforming the baseline (PeskaVLP) by 24.85\%-43.17\% in accuracy and even surpassing fully supervised linear probing and several state-of-the-art few-shot approaches. Codes will be released at https://github.com/YIYIZH/LaST.

</details>


### [125] [FOVEA: Focused On-Demand Visual Evidence Adaptation for Cache-Friendly Multimodal Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22883)
*Hengjie Zhu,Dayan Wu,Zihao Zhang,Xinze Liu,Jingxuan Yu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Ding Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FOVEA：在多模态推测解码中按需检索“视觉证据”，以轻量校正草稿模型隐藏态，而非在自回归上下文插入视觉token，从而在多基座与基准上提升草稿接受率与端到端解码速度（最高约2.13×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码常用固定视觉接口（固定视觉token预算或静态压缩表示），但视觉需求随任务与解码阶段显著波动：证据不足会削弱视觉对齐，过多上下文带来开销且扰乱草稿预测。因此需要一种能按状态自适应地提供刚好足够的视觉信息的方法。

Method: 构建可复用的视觉记忆缓存；在每个草稿状态上用累计质量（cumulative-mass）规则选择一个有界子集作为“视觉证据”；将所选条目聚合为视觉读出，并通过轻量门控残差校正（不向自回归序列插入视觉token）来仅修改传入LM头的表示；该流程与多种视觉-语言主干兼容、cache-friendly。

Result: 在多种VLM主干与多模态基准上，FOVEA提升草稿接受率并显著加速端到端解码，相比纯自回归最高达2.13×速度提升，同时保持或改善质量。

Conclusion: 面向状态的按需证据检索是复用固定视觉表示之外的有效替代方案；通过聚焦且受限的视觉读出对草稿态进行校正，可在不增加上下文长度的情况下提升多模态推测解码效率与稳定性。

Abstract: Multimodal speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models by allowing a lightweight draft model to propose candidate tokens for parallel verification by a larger target model. Existing methods typically condition the drafter on a fixed visual interface, such as a predefined visual-token budget or a static compressed representation. However, our controlled visual-budget analysis shows that visual demand varies substantially across tasks and decoding stages, which means more visual input is not always beneficial. Actually, insufficient evidence may weaken visual grounding, while excessive context adds overhead and may disrupt drafting. We propose FOVEA (Focused On-demand Visual Evidence Adaptation), a cache-friendly approach that builds a reusable visual memory and dynamically retrieves a bounded subset for a draft state. A cumulative-mass rule determines both how many and which entries are selected. The selected entries are aggregated into a visual readout and fused with the current draft hidden state through a lightweight gated residual correction. Rather than inserting visual tokens into the autoregressive context, the correction modifies only the representation passed to the language-model head. Experiments across multiple vision-language backbones and multimodal benchmarks show that FOVEA improves draft acceptance and end-to-end decoding speed, achieving up to $2.13\times$ speedup over autoregressive decoding. These results demonstrate that state-conditioned evidence retrieval is an effective alternative to reusing a fixed visual representation throughout multimodal generation.

</details>


### [126] [DRAgent: Discriminative Reasoning Agent for Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22885)
*Yujie Qi,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRAgent：用MLLM做判别式推理而非直接输出坐标，先检测候选、两阶段筛选目标，再用分割基础模型生成掩膜；并用自一致性过滤的推理链数据做LoRA微调；在RefCOCO/+/g上具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的RES常把连续空间位置离散成文本坐标一次性生成，易产生定位偏差与模态对齐误差，需要一种更稳健、可解释、减少坐标生成依赖的定位方式。

Method: 1) 由通用检测器产生高召回候选框空间；2) 将MLLM作为视觉-语义判别器，不负责回归坐标：— 阶段一：基于表达对候选进行高召回筛选；— 阶段二：实例级逐一核验，区分干扰项并选定目标；3) 将选中框作为空间提示输入基础分割模型生成像素级掩膜；4) 构建自一致性过滤的推理链数据，用LoRA微调MLLM以增强判别式推理能力。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg上达到与现有方法相当或更优的性能（文中称“competitive”），验证了框选+判别+分割的有效性。

Conclusion: 用“检测候选+MLLM判别推理+分割模型”替代MLLM直接坐标生成，可减轻离散化带来的定位偏差并提高跨模态对齐与鲁棒性；自一致性筛选的推理链微调进一步增强判别能力，整体获得强竞争力的RES表现。

Abstract: Referring Expression Segmentation (RES) aims to generate a pixel-level mask for the object specified by a language expression. Recent methods based on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often rely on one-pass coordinate prediction for visual localization, which serializes continuous spatial locations as discrete text tokens and may lead to localization bias and alignment errors. To address these issues, we propose DRAgent, an MLLM-driven discriminative reasoning (DR) framework for RES. Instead of requiring the MLLM to generate localization coordinates, DRAgent first constructs a detector-generated candidate space and then uses the MLLM as a visual-semantic target discriminator. Specifically, the MLLM performs reliable target selection among potential distractors through a two-stage DR mechanism, which first screens high-recall candidates and then performs instance-wise verification. The selected target box is subsequently used as a spatial prompt for a foundation segmentation model to produce the final pixel-level mask. Furthermore, we construct a self-consistency-filtered reasoning-chain data pipeline for LoRA-based fine-tuning, providing more reliable supervision for enhancing the MLLM's discriminative reasoning capability. Experiments demonstrate that DRAgent achieves competitive performance on RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and RefCOCOg.

</details>


### [127] [NemoSplat: Feed-Forward 4D Gaussian Splatting for Media-Aware Underwater Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22888)
*Xiaopeng Guo,Wai Chung Tse,Yipeng Zhu,Hanwen Zhang,Huajian Huang,Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NemoSplat：首个针对无标定海洋视频的媒体感知型前馈4D高斯点云重建框架，稳健解算位姿与深度，借助可提示的动态解耦与媒体感知高斯预测对抗散射与动态干扰，在新建大规模水下数据集上实现SOTA跟踪与高保真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用前馈新视角/跟踪模型在水下环境中因光学衰减、散射和大量不可预测动态体导致特征聚合失效，进而引发跟踪与重建崩溃；缺乏面向水下介质物理与动态扰动的统一前馈重建框架与数据集。

Method: 1) 前馈4D Gaussian Splatting：直接从未标定的海洋视频联合估计相机姿态与稠密深度并进行动态场重建。2) Promptable Dynamic Disentangler：融合学习到的动态概率与可选文本语义先验，以置信感知方式隔离大规模瞬态/动态实体。3) Media-Aware Gaussian Predictor：在一次前向中联合回归高斯属性与介质物理参数（如衰减/散射），恢复去劣化外观。4) 自建大规模水下动态数据集用于训练与评测。

Result: 在新数据集上，NemoSplat取得最先进的跟踪精度与高保真渲染效果，较现有前馈方法显著缓解因散射与动态造成的失败。

Conclusion: 结合动态解耦与媒体物理建模的前馈4D GS可在无标定水下视频中实现稳健的动态重建与渲染；数据集促进该方向研究。

Abstract: Reconstructing photorealistic scenes in unconstrained underwater environments remains challenging due to severe media-induced light scattering and unpredictable dynamic objects. Recent feed-forward visual foundation models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in generalized novel view synthesis and tracking. However, when directly applied to aquatic videos, optical attenuation and motion interference fatally corrupt their feature aggregation, leading to severe tracking and reconstruction failures. To overcome these limitations, we present NemoSplat, the first feed-forward 4D Gaussian Splatting framework tailored for media-aware dynamic reconstruction directly from uncalibrated marine videos. Beyond providing robust estimations of camera poses and dense scene depth, we devise a Promptable Dynamic Disentangler that utilizes a confidence-aware fusion strategy of learned dynamic probabilities and optional semantic text priors, effectively isolating massive transient entities. Furthermore, to counteract visual degradation, a Media-Aware Gaussian Predictor is formulated to jointly estimate intrinsic 3D Gaussian attributes alongside physical media parameters, rendering pristine scene appearance in a single forward pass. Additionally, we introduce a large-scale underwater dataset with massive dynamic elements to facilitate training and evaluation. Extensive experiments on our dataset demonstrate that NemoSplat achieves state-of-the-art tracking accuracy and high-fidelity rendering.

</details>


### [128] [AquaFlow: A Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Underwater Streaming Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22906)
*Yingxiang Xu,Kerui Ren,Wenqi Guo,Changjian Jiang,Tao Lu,Linning Xu,Mulin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AquaFlow提出面向水下场景的单目3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)流式重建框架，通过更稳健的位姿/点图估计、介质引导的增量高斯初始化与结合物理光学模型的混合表示，实现高效高保真重建，在62条多样水下轨迹上刷新SOTA：定位误差降13.2%，PSNR提升4.74 dB（较WaterSplat-SLAM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目3DGS流式重建在常规场景有效，但在水下受到强衰减与散射导致的图像退化影响，位姿跟踪不稳、几何失真，现有方法难以直接扩展。需要一个既能适应水下成像物理、又能保持在线/流式效率与质量的方案。

Method: 1) 以大规模水下数据微调3D视觉基础模型，获得鲁棒的相机位姿与点图(pointmap)估计；2) 设计介质(中等)引导的增量式高斯初始化策略，用于流式建图稳健启动与更新；3) 提出与流式兼容的混合场景表示：将结构化、距离调制的神经高斯与受物理启发的水下成像光学模型融合，补偿衰减与散射效应，提升几何与外观重建；4) 在多源水下序列上进行端到端流式重建。

Result: 在62条来自公开基准与网络实拍、不同尺度的水下轨迹上评测，相比WaterSplat-SLAM，平均定位误差降低13.2%，渲染PSNR提升4.74 dB；同时实现SOTA的跟踪与渲染表现。

Conclusion: 针对水下退化导致的位姿与几何失真，AquaFlow通过基础模型微调、介质感知的增量高斯初始化与物理融合的混合表示，实现鲁棒的单目3DGS流式重建，显著优于现有水下方法并具备高效在线重建能力。

Abstract: Recent monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) streaming reconstruction methods have achieved impressive performance by balancing reconstruction quality and efficiency. However, extending these frameworks to underwater scenes remains challenging due to severe visual degradation, such as light attenuation and scattering, which degrades camera pose tracking and distorts scene geometry. To address these challenges, we propose AquaFlow, a monocular Gaussian Splatting streaming reconstruction framework for efficient and high-fidelity underwater reconstruction. Specifically, AquaFlow fine-tunes a 3D vision foundation model on large-scale underwater data for robust pose and pointmap estimation, and introduces a medium-guided incremental Gaussian initialization strategy for streaming mapping. Furthermore, we develop a streaming-compatible hybrid scene representation that integrates structured, distance-conditioned neural Gaussians with a physics-inspired optical model to compensate for underwater image formation effects, enabling accurate scene reconstruction. We evaluate AquaFlow on a comprehensive dataset of 62 diverse underwater trajectories, collected from both public benchmarks and in-the-wild web videos across various scales. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AquaFlow achieves state-of-the-art tracking and rendering performance, reducing average localization error by 13.2% and improving PSNR by 4.74 dB compared to WaterSplat-SLAM.

</details>


### [129] [Results of the 1st Asynchronous CASTLE Challenge at the Joint Egocentric Vision Workshop in Conjunction with CVPR 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22914)
*Luca Rossetto,Werner Bailer,Cathal Gurrin,Graham Healy,Omar Shahbaz Khan,Stevan Rudinac,Klaus Schöffmann,Allie Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 报告概述了在CVPR 2026联合的第一届异步CASTLE挑战赛的贡献与成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动自我中心（第一视角）视觉领域中与“异步”设定相关的问题研究与基准评测，汇聚社区力量、标准化任务与评估，并展示最新方法与实践。

Method: 组织比赛与基准：定义异步CASTLE挑战的任务设置、数据与评估协议；收集参赛队伍的方法提交与结果；在研讨会上进行结果汇总与比较分析。

Result: 提供了参赛团队的成绩汇总、方法对比以及在异步自我中心视觉任务上的性能基线与排名（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 挑战赛成功搭建了评测平台并汇总首届成果，为后续研究与竞赛迭代提供了数据基线与经验教训，但摘要未包含具体技术细节或定量结果。

Abstract: This report summarizes the contributions and results of the 1st Asynchronous CASTLE Challenge at the Joint Egocentric Vision Workshop in conjunction with CVPR 2026.

</details>


### [130] [Motion-Based Tokenization for Cross-Dataset Egocentric Gaze Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22926)
*Virmarie Maquiling,Zhuojiang Cai,Enkelejda Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出以“事件对齐、固定视界的角位移”作为眼动表示，并通过多维评测比较其与多种替代表示（仅事件、空间/绝对角、VQ离散、连续）在可预测性与跨数据集迁移上的表现。结果显示运动化离散token在部分迁移方向上优于冻结码本的VQ表示，且事件构造方式显著影响迁移结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动正成为视觉与多模态模型的重要输入，但跨数据集的表示缺乏统一：原始轨迹噪声大、依赖设备；粗粒度事件标签易于建模却丢失局部运动结构。需要一种既可解释又便于跨数据集迁移的紧凑表示，并系统评估不同表示在预测性、信息保持与迁移上的折中。

Method: 提出“事件对齐、固定视界的角位移”作为事件条件下的运动词表（tokenization），并与六类表示对比：仅事件、空间位置、绝对角、学习的向量量化（VQ）离散token、连续表示等。评估框架结合：下一token预测、目标域regret、低阶目标参考、配对bootstrap、序敏性分析、motif重叠、冻结结构探针；并在头显事件对齐基准与第三个以自我视角数据集上做匹配比较（I-VT、原生事件、frame-span接口）。

Result: 在事件对齐头显基准中，角运动token在一个迁移方向上的目标域regret低于冻结码本VQ token，反向方向结论不明确；探针显示不同表示互补，仅事件token虽困惑度低但运动信息保留少。于第三个自我视角数据集，比较显示事件构造强烈影响迁移：原生事件迁入EGTEA时regret最低；frame-span事件与目标的motif零重叠，作为源域迁移严重失效。

Conclusion: 基于运动的事件对齐角位移token为自我视角注视流提供了紧凑且可解释的表示；评测框架揭示目标可预测性与事件构造会显著塑造跨数据集结论，提示选择与构造事件接口对迁移性能至关重要。

Abstract: Gaze is increasingly used as an input signal for vision and multimodal models, yet no consensus exists on how to represent it across datasets. Raw traces preserve detail but are noisy and device-dependent, while coarse event labels are easy to model but can discard local motion structure. We formulate event-aligned, fixed-horizon angular displacement as an interpretable, event-conditioned motion vocabulary and compare it with event-only, spatial, absolute-angle, learned vector-quantized, and continuous representations. To assess transfer alongside target predictability and token collapse, our evaluation combines next-token prediction with target-domain regret, low-order target references, paired bootstrap, order sensitivity, motif overlap, and frozen structural probes. In an event-aligned headset benchmark, angular-motion tokens have lower target-domain regret than frozen-codebook VQ tokens in one transfer direction, while the reverse direction is inconclusive. The probes reveal complementary representation properties, and event-only tokens show that low perplexity can retain little motion information. On a third egocentric dataset, a matched comparison of I-VT, native, and frame-span interfaces shows that event construction materially changes transfer: native events have the lowest regret into EGTEA, while frame-span events have zero motif overlap and fail severely as a source. Motion-based tokenization therefore provides a compact representation for event-aligned egocentric gaze streams, while the evaluation identifies how target predictability and event construction shape cross-dataset conclusions.

</details>


### [131] [Quality Inspection of Printed Circuit Board Pin Insertion via Semantic Segmentation and Board-Level Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22937)
*Nils Rabeneck,André Kiunke,Nicole Hoess,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于PCB插针错位检测的自动化方法：先用U-Net做语义分割得到引脚掩膜，再做轮廓特征提取，最后用逻辑回归实现板级合格/不合格判定；在工业与公共数据集上分别达成0.990与1.000的ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配过程中引脚错位会导致产品不可靠，但在插针过程中或之后的快速准确检测仍然困难。现有方法（如通用异常检测或实例分割）在稳定性、可迁移性或成本上存在不足，因此需要一种鲁棒、可解释、适用于工业环境的自动化检测方案。

Method: 构建分层流水线：1) 采用U-Net进行语义分割获取每个引脚的二值掩膜；2) 从掩膜中提取引脚轮廓并计算板级统计特征（如平均轮廓尺寸等）；3) 使用逻辑回归基于这些特征进行板级通过/失败分类；4) 在两个风格差异显著的数据集上训练与评估，并与PatchCore异常检测和基于实例分割的检测方法对比。

Result: 在工业真实PCB图像的随机划分测试集上，ROC-AUC=0.990；在公开数据集上，ROC-AUC=1.000，实现对合格与不合格板的强分离；相较PatchCore与实例分割基线表现更优或具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于U-Net分割+轮廓特征+逻辑回归的流水线能在不同视觉风格的数据集上（经数据集特定训练后）实现高精度的引脚错位检测，是工业自动化引脚检测的有前景方案。

Abstract: Quality control during printed circuit board (PCB) assembly is a critical step in ensuring reliable electronic products. Detecting misaligned pins during or after pin insertion remains a particularly challenging inspection task. This paper presents an automated defect detection method for identifying incorrectly inserted pins on PCBs. The proposed pipeline combines semantic segmentation using a U-Net architecture with contour-based feature extraction and logistic regression for board-level pass/fail classification. Segmentation masks are used to derive contour representations of individual pins, from which board-level features -such as average contour size- are extracted and used to train a logistic regression classifier. We evaluate the method on two datasets: an industrial collection of real-world PCB images, and a publicly available PCB pin-inspection dataset with substantially different visual characteristics. To assess the effectiveness of the proposed approach, a comparison against PatchCore, an anomaly detection technique new to be applied to pin inspection, as well as instance segmentation-based pin detection is made. The developed method achieved Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve (ROC-AUC) values of 0.990 on a random test set split from the industrial data and 1.000 on the public dataset indicating strong separation between pass and fail boards. The results indicate that the proposed approach is a promising candidate for automated pin inspection in industrial environments and achieves strong performance on datasets with substantially different visual characteristics after dataset-specific training.

</details>


### [132] [WADE: A Reasoning-Annotated Benchmark for Multi-Instance Floating-Waste Grounding with Compact Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22950)
*Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Ahsan Farabi,Md. Abdul Ahad Minhaz,Mahedi Hasan,Israt Khandaker,Ibrahim Khalil Shanto,Muhammad Nomani Kabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WADE数据集与评测基准，用于在杂乱多目标内陆水域中对漂浮垃圾进行定位、分类、计数与可解释推理；并展示在紧凑型VLM上经QLoRA联合微调后显著降低幻觉、提升召回与F1，但任务仍具高难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水域垃圾数据集地理覆盖有限、多实例标注稀疏、监督信号单一（仅框与标签），导致紧凑型视觉-语言模型在联合定位/分类/计数/解释上的评估与能力不足；需要一个带推理标注的基准来推动资源受限条件下的可靠监测。

Method: 构建WADE：来自孟加拉农村的2,167张图像、13,608个框、10类垃圾；每个实例附带类别层面的识别规则（视觉线索、易混淆项、判别特征）。在零样本、两样本、推理引导与微调四种设置下评测6个VLM，指标涵盖检测、计数与幻觉。为高效自适应，使用QLoRA对Qwen3-VL-2B进行联合微调（框、标签与推理链）。

Result: 联合微调后，Qwen3-VL-2B召回从0.0248升至0.2339，F1从0.0257升至0.2163；图像级幻觉率从0.6836降至0.0883。尽管如此，超过3/4的实例仍未被检测到。

Conclusion: WADE为紧凑型VLM在密集漂浮垃圾定位上的困难基准；带推理监督与QLoRA的联合微调可显著提升检测与减少幻觉，但仍存在大规模漏检，提示需进一步方法与数据改进。

Abstract: Floating waste in inland waterways threatens aquatic ecosystems and requires timely monitoring under cluttered, multi-object conditions. Existing aquatic-waste datasets provide limited geographic coverage, sparse multi-instance annotations, and little supervision beyond boxes and labels. Compact vision-language models (VLMs) therefore remain insufficiently evaluated for jointly localizing, classifying, counting, and explaining floating waste. We introduce WADE, a reasoning-annotated benchmark containing 2,167 images from rural Bangladesh, 13,608 bounding boxes, and ten waste categories. Each annotation is associated with class-level recognition rules covering visual cues, likely confusions, and discriminative features. We evaluate six VLMs under zero-shot, two-shot, reasoning-guided, and fine-tuned settings using detection, counting, and hallucination metrics. For resource-efficient adaptation, we jointly fine-tune Qwen3-VL-2B on boxes, labels, and reasoning chains using QLoRA. Fine-tuning increases recall from 0.0248 to 0.2339 and F1 from 0.0257 to 0.2163, while reducing image-level hallucination from 0.6836 to 0.0883. However, over three-quarters of instances remain undetected, establishing WADE as a challenging benchmark for dense floating-waste grounding with compact VLMs.

</details>


### [133] [WildHandBench: A Benchmark for Handwritten Text Understanding that Challenges MLLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22959)
*Jun Zhang,Qiao Zhao,Cheng Cui,Jianying Qu,Zhongkai Sun,Jianwen Yang,Changda Zhou,ZhuoXin Liu,Shubin Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildHandBench提出手写文档解析基准，涵盖多结构、多语言与真实退化场景，并引入PDE指标揭示模型依赖语言先验的问题；SOTA仅71.85%，人类77.09%，模型错误多为先验驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文档解析在印刷体上接近饱和，但对手写文档能力缺乏系统评估。既有基准忽视手写表格与真实退化，仅给出总体准确率，无法解释模型为何失败。需要一个覆盖更广场景并能区分错误成因的评测框架。

Method: 构建WildHandBench：500份手写文档，涵盖自由文本、表格、公式三种结构，四种语言，九类真实场景退化。设计Prior-Driven Error（PDE）度量，用于量化错误是否源自语言先验而非视觉证据。评测18个SOTA模型并设置经校准的人类基线。

Result: 最佳模型总体仅71.85%；人类表现为77.09%，优于模型但差距不大；模型错误与人类错误分布不同，模型中63%-91%的错误为先验驱动，而人类为49%。

Conclusion: 现有模型在手写文档解析上远未解决，且过度依赖语言先验，传统准确率无法揭示此缺陷。WildHandBench与PDE为分析与改进手写文档解析模型提供了更全面、可解释的评测框架。

Abstract: While the top model on OmniDocBench now reaches 96.34% overall on printed-document parsing, the ability of current models to handle challenging handwritten documents remains largely uncharacterized. Existing benchmarks focus on isolated text or formulas, overlook handwritten tables and real-world degradation, and report aggregate accuracy without explaining why models fail.
  We present WildHandBench, a benchmark containing 500 handwritten documents across three structures (free text, tables, formulas), four languages, and nine real-world scenarios. We introduce a Prior-Driven Error (PDE) metric that quantifies whether errors originate from language priors rather than visual evidence. Evaluating 18 state-of-the-art models together with calibrated human baselines, we find: (1) the best model achieves only 71.85% overall; (2) humans outperform all models yet the gap is narrow (77.09% vs. 71.85%); and (3) model errors are qualitatively different from human errors -- 63-91% of model errors are prior-driven versus only 49% for humans, exposing systematic reliance on language priors that conventional accuracy metrics cannot capture.

</details>


### [134] [Simplified Cross-Modal Calibration for Heterogeneous Event-RGB Stereo Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22965)
*Nico Hessenthaler,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需运动的事件相机与帧相机外参标定方法：用在普通显示器上交替呈现原始与部分混合的ChArUco图案触发事件，同时保证帧相机可见；将事件粗配准成帧后与RGB图像一起进行轻量去噪与ChArUco标定，最终获得更低重投影误差并简化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构立体系统（事件相机+帧相机）的外参标定困难；现有方法常依赖目标/传感器运动、严格同步或昂贵的事件重建，给实验和部署带来复杂性与不稳定性。

Method: 在消费级显示器上展示时域调制的混合ChArUco靶标：在原始与部分透明混合图案间交替切换，使事件相机产生事件而帧相机持续观测到清晰图案；以触发信号实现粗同步。将事件按时间窗离散成与RGB图像粗对齐的事件帧，做轻量去噪，然后用ChArUco执行内参与双目外参标定。系统性实验评估混合不透明度、显示亮度、外部光照、视角与手持采集等因素的鲁棒性。

Result: 相较于E2Calib+Kalibr（基于运动的强基线）与Plasberg等（非运动基线），本方法将平均重投影误差分别降低44%与6%，并显著简化标定流程；在机器人手眼标定案例中，获得一致的位姿变换与在部分遮挡下仍稳定的几何测量。

Conclusion: 通过时域调制的混合ChArUco与事件离散化，本方法在无需运动与严格同步的条件下，实现简单、鲁棒、精度更高的事件-帧相机外参标定，并在实际机器人应用中验证了实用性。

Abstract: Accurate extrinsic calibration between event-based and frame-based cameras remains a practical bottleneck for heterogeneous stereo systems. Existing approaches often require sensor or target motion, precise synchronization, or computationally expensive event-to-image reconstruction. We propose a simple, motion-free cross-modal calibration framework that uses a temporally modulated, blended ChArUco target presented on standard consumer displays. By alternating between the original pattern and a partially blended version, the target reliably triggers events while remaining continuously observable to a frame-based camera, avoiding blank frames and reducing synchronization constraints to a coarse, trigger-based alignment. We discretize events into frames coarsely aligned with the RGB images, apply lightweight denoising, and perform ChArUco-based intrinsic and stereo extrinsic calibration. Extensive experiments assess robustness to blending opacity, display brightness, external illumination, viewing angle, and handheld acquisition. Compared to the strongest motion-based reference (E2Calib + Kalibr) and a non-motion-based reference (Plasberg et al.), our approach reduces the mean reprojection error by $44\%$ and $6\%$, respectively, while substantially simplifying the calibration procedure. Finally, we demonstrate practical utility in a robotic eye-to-hand calibration case study, showing consistent transformations and stable downstream geometric measurements even under partial occlusions. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/nhessenthaler/simple-evrgb-cal.

</details>


### [135] [Optimize Surgical Triplet Recognition: A Knowledge-Driven Mixture-of-Experts Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22972)
*Yiyi Zhang,Yuchen Yuan,Ying Zheng,Jialun Pei,Jinpeng Li,Zheng Li,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoeCo框架用于手术动作三元组（器械-动词-目标）识别，通过组件解耦、梯度协调与知识驱动的MoE整合多模态LLM先验，缓解特征与类别冲突并提升鲁棒性；在CholecT45/50上取得优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在复杂手术场景中难以准确建模器械、动词、目标及其关联，主要受限于：1) 组件级（instrument/verb/target）特征空间纠缠导致优化冲突；2) 类别级严重长尾失衡造成正负梯度不均；3) 缺少可解释的领域知识引导，影响鲁棒性与泛化。

Method: 提出知识驱动的Mixture-of-Experts-guided Co-Optimization（MoeCo）。(a) 组件定制适配器：在时空维度上解耦并专门化三类组件特征；(b) 协同梯度学习：自适应重加权正负梯度，聚焦稀有类别；(c) 知识驱动MoE：引入多模态大语言模型生成的外部手术先验，通过专家激活动态融合到编码表示中，形成更具表达力与鲁棒性的联合表示与联合优化流程。

Result: 在CholecT45与CholecT50上进行大量实验，MoeCo的整体三元组识别性能优于现有方法；同时验证了协同优化管线与知识驱动MoE动态先验整合的有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过组件解耦与梯度协调缓解优化冲突，并以知识驱动的MoE动态注入领域先验，MoeCo显著提升手术动作三元组识别的准确性、可解释性与鲁棒性，在公开数据集上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Surgical action triplet recognition constitutes a critical task in context-aware robot-assisted surgery, facilitating automatic surgical action perception by identifying instrument, verb, target, and their association. However, existing works struggle to analyze such complex surgical scenes due to three main issues: (1) component-level optimization conflicts caused by entangled feature spaces, (2) category-level optimization conflicts arising from severe data imbalance, and (3) lack of domain knowledge guidance that limits model interpretability and robustness. To address these challenges, we propose a Mixture-of-Experts-guided Co-Optimization (\textit{MoeCo}) framework powered by knowledge-driven learning. Within the co-optimization pipeline, to first mitigate component-level conflicts, we introduce a component-tailored adapter that disentangles task-specific features across spatial-temporal regimes, facilitating effective component specialization. Next, we develop a coordinated gradient learning strategy to handle category-level conflicts, which adaptively rebalances positive-negative gradients to enhance the perception of rare categories. Notably, inspired by surgical domain expertise, we introduce a knowledge-driven mixture-of-experts mechanism that dynamically integrates multimodal large language model-guided knowledge via activated experts, thereby enriching the co-optimization pipeline with more expressive and robust representations. Extensive experiments on the public CholecT45 and CholecT50 datasets confirm the effectiveness of the proposed co-optimization pipeline and the superiority of dynamic priors integration via the knowledge-driven mixture-of-experts mechanism.

</details>


### [136] [ENCORE: Entropy-Guided Cropping and Attention Regularization for Robust Vision--Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22996)
*Yuanhao Sun,Huawei Ji,Jiaxin Ding,Luoyi Fu,Xinbing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ENCORE：基于跨模态注意力熵的裁剪与训练正则，动态保留与提示相关的连续区域，使轻量VLM在多VQA基准上以极少参数微调获得SOTA与+1.43%平均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级VLM常用固定分块/分辨率视觉编码，导致目标被切割、与提示相关区域的完整性受损；现有方法多只在视觉侧做静态处理，缺乏依据文本提示动态选择关键区域的机制。作者观察到早期层图文交叉注意力的熵与答案定位质量和任务准确率强相关，启发用“低熵=高确定性/更聚焦”的信号来指导区域选择与训练。

Method: 提出ENCORE，包括两部分：1) 推理阶段的Entropy-based Cropping Strategy (ECS)：生成若干候选裁剪，对早期层跨模态注意力的图文熵进行评估，选择熵最小的裁剪以保留与提示相关的连续视觉区域；2) 训练阶段的Entropy Regularization Training (ERT)：在常规下一词预测目标上加入熵正则，鼓励注意力在关键视觉token上更尖锐、降低对无关token的权重。整体仅微调约0.14%的参数，适配2B级VLM。

Result: 在十个VQA数据集上，平均准确率提升1.43%，在近期约20亿参数的VLM中达到SOTA；同时证明早期层跨模态注意力熵与答案定位/准确率高度相关，ECS与ERT均有贡献。

Conclusion: 跨模态注意力熵可作为可靠的动态区域选择与注意力整形信号。ENCORE以微小参数开销在轻量VLM上显著提升VQA表现，兼顾推理时的提示相关区域完整性与训练时的注意力聚焦。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well on diverse vision-language tasks, but transformer-based visual encoders split images into fixed-resolution sub-images, compromising object integrity in lightweight VLMs. Existing methods only focus on the visual modality and fail to dynamically preserve the integrity of prompt-relevant regions, limiting performance. In this work, we observe that the early-layer image-text entropy of cross-modal attention strongly correlates with answer grounding quality and task accuracy. Building on this finding, we propose \textbf{ENCORE}, an entropy-guided framework with two components: At inference, an \textbf{Entropy-based Cropping Strategy} (ECS) evaluates a small set of candidate crops and selects the one with minimal entropy, preserving contiguous regions relevant to the prompt. At training, \textbf{Entropy Regularization Training} (ERT) augments next-token prediction with an entropy term that sharpens attention on key visual tokens while down-weighting irrelevant ones. Experiments on ten VQA benchmarks show that ENCORE, fine-tuning only 0.14\% of parameters, achieves an average 1.43\% accuracy gain and state-of-the-art performance among recent 2B-parameter VLMs. Our code is released in https://github.com/baokou-fw2/ENCORE.

</details>


### [137] [Coarse Indexing, Fine Evidence: Decoupling Temporal Granularity in Long-Video RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23011)
*Zhe Jin,Zhimin Lin,Bin Zheng,Junhua Fang,Huihua Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于长视频RAG的训练-free方法DAGC：用密度自适应的粗粒度图索引进行检索，检索到的片段再还原到细粒度做证据推理；在保留约40–50%节点下，实现1.3–1.7×加速且几乎不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频RAG将索引粒度与证据粒度绑死：视频先按时间切块建图并用于检索与推理。实际上，定位相关时间段只需较粗表示，而最终回答仍需细证据。固定细粒度全程参与既冗余又耗时，限制了可扩展性。

Method: Density-Aware Graph Construction（DAGC）。不训练：1）基于相邻块的视觉冗余度进行密度自适应合并，构建紧凑的粗粒度图索引；2）保留从合并节点到原始时间块的映射；3）检索阶段先在粗索引上定位相关区域；4）将检索到的粗区域展开回原始细粒度块，进行证据精炼与答案生成。

Result: 在MLVU、VideoMME、LongVideoBench上：图节点仅为原始的约40–50%；端到端墙钟时间加速1.3–1.7×；问答性能约保留99%。收益可迁移至不同LVLM骨干与视频RAG流程。

Conclusion: 长视频RAG的索引与证据推理无需共享相同时间粒度。通过DAGC的密度自适应合并与映射，可在几乎不损精度的前提下显著压缩索引与加速推理，具备通用性与落地价值。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides a scalable paradigm for long-video understanding, but existing systems typically inherit a fixed temporal granularity from video segmentation when constructing their retrieval index. We argue that this design unnecessarily couples indexing granularity with evidence granularity: coarse representations can often suffice for locating relevant temporal regions, while fine-grained evidence remains important for downstream reasoning. We propose \textbf{Density-Aware Graph Construction (DAGC)}, a training-free approach that decouples a query-independent coarse retrieval index from the original fine-grained evidence space. DAGC constructs a compact, density-adaptive graph index by merging visually redundant neighboring chunks, while preserving mappings to the original temporal units. Retrieved coarse regions are subsequently expanded back to the original chunk granularity for fine-grained evidence refinement and answer generation. Experiments on MLVU, VideoMME, and LongVideoBench show that DAGC retains only about 40--50\% of the original graph nodes and achieves $1.3$--$1.7\times$ end-to-end wall-clock acceleration while preserving approximately 99\% of the original QA performance. The gains transfer across different LVLM backbones and video RAG pipelines, suggesting that long-video RAG need not maintain the same temporal granularity for indexing and evidence reasoning.

</details>


### [138] [Misanthrope: A Privacy-Preserving Keypoint Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23012)
*Francesco Vultaggio,Predrag Djindjic,Markus Gerke,Sebastian Tschiatschek,Phillipp Fanta-Jende*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Misanthrope：一种隐私友好的关键点检测器，通过自蒸馏学会避开人物区域选点，从源头缓解特征反演泄隐；在不牺牲匹配性能的同时，显著降低可被反演与重识别的人像信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地特征在SLAM/定位/SfM中关键，但会被特征反演攻击重建含隐私的场景，尤其在边端将特征上送服务器的分布式场景。现有多为后处理遮挡，难兼顾性能与隐私，需从“检测源头”规避敏感区域（如人物）。

Method: 提出Misanthrope关键点检测器：以自蒸馏训练，使学生模型模仿教师的高质量可匹配关键点，同时加入避免在人像区域产生关键点的约束与学习信号。利用由传统管线特征反演得到的图像作为训练/评估中对隐私风险的参照，促使模型对人物区域“厌恶”。无需显式人脸/人体识别推理即可在推理时规避人物选点。

Result: 在隐私方面：传统特征可被反演并用于检测与重识别场景中人物；Misanthrope显著减轻此类攻击风险。在性能方面：保持与SOTA相当的图像匹配质量，并在人物作为干扰的困难场景（如phototourism与野外里程计）中超越SOTA；在IMC 2021 Phototourism测试集中，9个场景中有7个成为最佳稀疏特征提取器。

Conclusion: 通过在关键点检测阶段规避人物区域，Misanthrope在不牺牲甚至提升匹配性能的同时，从源头提升隐私防护，对分布式视觉定位与SLAM等具有实用价值；代码与评测脚本已开源。

Abstract: Image matching is a core component of applications such as Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), Visual Localization, and Structure from Motion (SfM). However, the local image features central to this task are vulnerable to inversion attacks, which enable adversaries to reconstruct privacy-sensitive scene content from local features. These attacks pose a particular threat in distributed computing scenarios where the pre-computed features leave edge devices to be processed by remote servers. In this work, we introduce Misanthrope, a novel privacy-preserving keypoint detector trained through self-distillation to avoid detecting keypoints on people---a predominant source of privacy-sensitive content in most localization scenarios---thus mitigating inversion attacks at the source rather than through post-hoc obfuscation. We demonstrate how inverted images from traditional feature detection pipelines can be used to detect and re-identify people in the scene, while Misanthrope is able to mitigate these attacks. Furthermore, Misanthrope maintains image matching performance on par with the state of the art and even surpasses it in challenging settings where people act as distractors, such as phototourism and in-the-wild odometry. On the Image Matching Challenge 2021 Phototourism test set, Misanthrope is the top-performing sparse feature extractor in 7 out of 9 scenes. We make our model and its evaluation script available here: https://github.com/fratopa/misanthrope

</details>


### [139] [AnaDiffusion: Anatomically CompositionalLatent Diffusion for Controllable 3D Brain MRI Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23014)
*Huiwen Han,Lulin Liu,Bangya Liu,Yuanhao Cai,Nuo Chen,Xiaoqing Wang,Ziqian Xie,Chenyu You,Shuiwang Ji,Degui Zhi,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnaDiffusion是一种解剖学可组合的潜空间扩散框架，将3D脑MRI生成分解为解剖学部件的“先分后合”，先学局部先验、再组装并全局细化，实现可控的局部编辑且保持整体一致；在ADNI测试上取得最优/次优的FID与效应量指标，并在局部替换实验中保持高目标转移与低离靶改变。


<details>
